TL;DR: 本文针对经验采样法（ESM）纵向数据中因缺失数据导致的机器学习偏差问题，提出了一种新型神经网络架构——深度广义混合模型（Deep Generalised Mixed Model），结合变分自编码器和贝叶斯数据增强算法，实现半参数灵活建模，能够处理随机缺失（MAR）数据并提供有效推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源自GrowIt!应用在COVID-19疫情期间收集的青少年日常情绪ESM数据。分析此类数据面临两大挑战：一是标准统计技术难以扩展至高维情境，二是数据因参与者先前经历强烈负面情绪而退出，导致随机缺失（MAR），而标准机器学习方法无法适应这种缺失机制，会产生选择偏差。

Method: 作者开发了一种新颖的神经网络架构，将混合效应模型推广至深度学习框架。该模型通过固定效应和随机效应实现对数据均值和相关结构的半参数灵活建模。估计方法采用变分自编码器（VAE）的改进版本以及贝叶斯数据增强算法。此方法能够适应服从任意分布的纵向结局变量，可扩展至高维设定，并在MAR条件下提供有效推断。

Result: 将深度广义混合模型应用于GrowIt!研究及多种模拟实验。结果表明该方法具有一定潜力，但因模型不稳定性导致性能尚未达到最优。

Conclusion: 深度广义混合模型为处理ESM中高维、随机缺失的纵向数据提供了一条有前景的路径，但模型稳定性问题亟待进一步解决，方能实现其全部潜力。

Abstract: The experience sampling method (ESM) is a longitudinal research design where participants report their thoughts, emotional states and behaviours multiple times a day. Our work is motivated by such data collected by the GrowIt! app, which was released to investigate daily emotions among adolescents during the COVID-19 pandemic. Current procedures to analyse ESM data face various challenges. While standard statistical techniques may not scale well to a high-dimensional setting, machine learning procedures can give biased results due to selection bias introduced by missingness. In our motivating dataset, adolescents dropped out due to previous strong feelings of negative emotions. Hence, the implied missing data are of the missing-at-random type that standard machine learning procedures cannot accommodate. We develop a novel neural network architecture that generalises mixed effects models to deep learning to overcome these challenges. It allows semi-parametric and flexible modelling of data's mean and correlation structure through fixed and random effects. For estimation, we use an adaptation of variational auto-encoders and a Bayesian data augmentation algorithm. Through this approach, the model can accommodate longitudinal outcomes following generic distributions, scale well to high-dimensional settings and provide valid inference when data are missing-at-random. We applied the Deep Generalised Mixed Model to the GrowIt! study and various simulations. The results show potential for the Deep Generalised Mixed Model, yet suboptimal performance due to model instability.

</details>


### [249] [Handling Missing Data in Probabilistic Regression Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06195)
*Taiane Schaedler Prass,Alisson Silva Neimaier,Guilherme Pumi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: PRTrees 被扩展为在树构建过程中直接处理缺失预测变量，提出三种策略（均匀概率法、部分观测法、降维平滑法），在保留原有概率特性的同时，在含大量缺失值的数据集上常优于 CART，且填充策略对预测性能的影响最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概率回归树（PRTrees）无法直接处理缺失预测变量，需要预先插补。本文旨在扩展 PRTree 框架，使其在树构建过程中原生支持缺失值，避免先验插补带来的偏差和信息损失。

Method: 提出三种缺失值处理策略：(1) 均匀概率法——对缺失维度分配均匀概率；(2) 部分观测法——仅基于已观测维度计算分裂概率；(3) 降维平滑法——在降维后的特征空间中进行概率平滑。三种方法均保证在任意缺失模式下维持概率守恒和边缘相容性。在多个不同缺失率的真实数据集上与 CART 对比评估。

Result: 填充策略是主导建模成分，对预测性能的影响超过平滑分布和代理选择准则。在含有大量缺失预测变量的数据集中，所提方法在保持树模型可解释性和灵活性的同时，经常优于 CART。

Conclusion: PRTrees 可直接处理缺失值的扩展是有效的，填充策略的选择至关重要。该方法在缺失数据场景下兼具预测性能与模型可解释性，为回归树处理缺失值提供了新范式。

Abstract: Probabilistic Regression Trees (PRTrees) are a smooth and consistent alternative to classical regression trees, producing continuous predictions through probabilistic split assignments. This paper extends the PRTree framework to accommodate missing predictor values directly during tree construction, eliminating the need for prior imputation. Three strategies are proposed, each exploiting the available information differently: a uniform-probability approach, a partial-observation approach, and a dimension-reduced smoothing approach. These modifications are defined to preserve the fundamental probabilistic properties of the original methodology, including probability conservation and marginal compatibility, under arbitrary patterns of missing covariate values. The proposed methods are evaluated on several real-world datasets exhibiting different levels of missingness and are compared with classical regression trees. The results show that the effectiveness of probabilistic tree construction depends strongly on the treatment of missing observations. Across the considered datasets, the fill strategy emerged as the dominant modeling component, often exerting a larger influence on predictive performance than either the smoothing distribution or the proxy-selection criterion. In datasets where a substantial proportion of observations contained missing predictor values, the proposed methods frequently outperformed CART, while maintaining the interpretability and flexibility of tree-based models.

</details>


### [250] [Beyond Marginal Validity: Finite-Sample Guarantees for Localized Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06206)
*Anton Conrad,Rustam Isaev,Denis Belomestny,Eric Moulines,Sergey Samsonov*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为随机局部共形预测（RLCP）提供了首个有限样本理论保证，在高概率下联合控制条件覆盖差距和相对预言器的长度误差，并分析了数据分割学习评分下的均匀局部保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测虽能保证有限样本的边际覆盖，但边际有效性可能掩盖严重的协变量特异性误校准，而精确的条件覆盖在有限样本下不可达。现有RLCP理论缺乏对实际局部集合的有限样本保证，无法联合控制条件有效性和预言器效率。

Method: 在Hölder正则性条件评分CDF、标准密度和核假设下，推导局部邻域上一致的高概率界。界分解为O(h^β)局部化偏差和随校准样本量递减的校准项，揭示带宽偏差-方差权衡。还分析数据分割学习评分情形（如共形分位数回归），将均匀局部保证分解为固定评分校准误差和评分估计误差。

Result: 证明了对任意固定评分，在实现的局部化邻域上一致成立的高概率界，明确刻画了RLCP跟踪预言器的条件。界由局部化偏差O(h^β)和校准项组成。对于学习评分，均匀局部保证可分解为固定评分校准和均匀评分估计误差之和，表明改进学习可锐化局部保证。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了RLCP有限样本理论空白，为局部化共形推断提供了严格的条件覆盖与效率保证，并揭示了带宽选择、校准样本量与评分学习质量之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: Conformal prediction endows arbitrary black-box predictors with finite-sample, distribution-free marginal coverage, yet marginal validity can hide severe covariate-specific miscalibration, while exact distribution-free conditional coverage is finite-sample unattainable. Randomly localized conformal prediction (RLCP) mitigates this gap by calibrating near the test point while preserving marginal coverage. Existing theory, however, lacks finite-sample guarantees for the realized localized set that jointly control conditional validity and oracle efficiency. We provide such guarantees. For any fixed score, under Hölder regularity of the conditional score CDF and standard density and kernel assumptions, we prove high-probability bounds, uniform over a realized localization neighbourhood, for the conditional-coverage gap and the length error relative to the oracle. The bounds decompose into an $O(h^β)$ localization bias and a calibration term decreasing with calibration size, clarifying the bandwidth bias-variance tradeoff and when RLCP tracks the oracle. We also analyze data-split learned scores: when the score targets a pivotal score, as in conformalized quantile regression, uniform local guarantees decompose into fixed-score calibration and uniform score-estimation errors, showing that improved learning sharpens localized guarantees.

</details>


### [251] [Minimax Optimal Early-Stopped Gradient Descent for Gaussian Mixture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06250)
*Alex Buna,Shirley Xiaoqi Liu,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 在过参数化分类中，梯度下降在逻辑损失上会发散但方向收敛到最大间隔插值分类器，其隐式偏差可能统计次优。本文证明早停法可克服此问题：在含标签翻转噪声的高斯混合模型中，适当早停的GD在协方差谱快速衰减（多项式/指数衰减）时达到极小极大最优超额0-1风险，并通过实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化设置下，梯度下降对逻辑损失会收敛到最大间隔解（隐式偏差），但该解在标签翻转噪声存在时可能统计次优。作者旨在探究早停法能否克服这种次优性，达到统计上的最优速率。

Method: 在含标签翻转噪声的高斯混合模型框架下分析早停梯度下降：1）推导早停迭代点的超额风险上界；2）构建任意分类器的匹配统计下界；3）提出新的校准结果，将超额逻辑风险转化为超额0-1风险，能处理标签噪声引起的模型误设，并消除了标准界中的平方根速率；4）建立线性插值器的下界，证明插值方法需指数级更多样本。

Result: 早停GD在适当oracle时刻停止时，对快速衰减和连续衰减的协方差谱（包括多项式和指数衰减）达到极小极大最优超额0-1风险；实验验证了理论速率的正确性；插值方法需远多于早停法的样本量才能达到相同的超额风险。

Conclusion: 早停法能有效克服过参数化逻辑回归中最大间隔解的隐式偏差带来的统计次优性，在标签翻转噪声的高斯混合模型下达到极小极大最优速率，并显著优于线性插值方法。

Abstract: In overparameterised classification, training data can be linearly separable even when the underlying distribution is not. In this setting, gradient descent (GD) on the logistic loss diverges in norm while converging in direction to a max-margin interpolating classifier, whose implicit bias can be statistically suboptimal. In this work, we show that early stopping can overcome this suboptimality: in a Gaussian mixture model with label-flipping noise, GD stopped at an appropriate oracle time achieves minimax-optimal excess zero-one risk for covariance spectra with fast and continuous decay, including polynomial and exponential spectral decays. Our analysis combines a sharp upper bound for the early-stopped iterate with a matching statistical lower bound over arbitrary classifiers, yielding optimal rates that are validated by experiments. A central technical contribution is a new calibration result that converts excess logistic risk into excess zero-one risk; it handles the model misspecification induced by the label-flipping noise, and removes the square-root rate in standard bounds. We also establish a lower bound for linear interpolators, showing that interpolation can require exponentially more samples than early stopping to achieve the same excess risk.

</details>


### [252] [Stochastic Dynamics on Persistence Diagram Space via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06276)
*Farzana Nasrin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的持续性图（PD）空间上的随机动力学框架，通过拓扑感知的局部编辑操作定义可变基数的受控马尔可夫过程，建立了几何遍历性和唯一平稳概率律的条件，实现了自适应拓扑简化和概率建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续性图的统计分析文献多将PD视为静态对象，缺乏PD空间上的概率建模和随机演化框架。作者希望填补这一空白，为PD空间引入动态演化机制，使其能够朝向科学相关的拓扑目标进行自适应简化。

Method: 提出强化学习框架，在PD空间上定义基于局部编辑操作（如点的添加、删除、移动）的受控马尔可夫过程。证明了在适当条件下马尔可夫链的不可约性、非周期性和几何遍历性，从而保证唯一平稳分布的存在。奖励函数设计综合了分布匹配、任务特定的拓扑统计量和结构保持压缩三个目标。

Result: 在合成数据和神经影像PD上的实验表明，该框架能够在显著降低图复杂度的同时，保持主导拓扑结构的完整性。

Conclusion: 该框架为PD空间提供了一种统一的概率建模与自适应拓扑简化方法，通过调节奖励函数可灵活平衡分布目标、图保真度和复杂度缩减，在理论上有平稳性保障，实验上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Persistence diagrams (PDs) provide stable and interpretable summaries of multiscale topological structure. While substantial progress has been made in the statistical analysis of PDs, existing literature often treats diagrams as static objects and provide limited frameworks for probabilistic modeling and stochastic evolution on PD space. We introduce a reinforcement learning framework for stochastic dynamics on PD space, where diagrams evolve through topology aware local edit operations. The dynamics define controlled Markov processes on spaces of finite PDs with variable cardinality. We establish conditions under which the induced Markov chains are irreducible, aperiodic, and geometrically ergodic, implying the existence of unique stationary probability laws on PD space. To guide the dynamics toward scientifically relevant topological targets, we formulate objectives that encompass distribution matching, task specific topological statistics, and structure-preserving compression. The resulting rewards balance task specific distributional targets, diagram fidelity, and complexity reduction, and yield a framework for adaptive topological simplification and probabilistic modeling. Experiments on synthetic and neuroimaging PDs demonstrate that the proposed framework can preserve dominant topological structure while reducing diagram complexity.

</details>


### [253] [Optimal Rates for Learning with Monotone Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06337)
*Anay Mehrotra*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了在单调对手插入正确标注样本的污染模型下，PAC学习的极小极大误差率。结果表明，对于VC维d≥2的假设类，额外对数因子是固有的，极小极大误差为Θ((d/n)log(n/d))，而非经典PAC的Θ(d/n)。令人意外的是，添加正确标注的样本反而使学习更难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Larsen等人提出的模型中，对手可观察i.i.d.干净样本后插入有限数量的正确标注样本，破坏数据的可交换性。此前已知经验风险最小化(ERM)在该模型下达到O((d/n)log(n/d))误差，但尚不清楚额外的对数因子是特定算法的缺陷还是不可交换性带来的固有代价。本文旨在回答这一根本性问题。

Method: 下界方面：构造一个显式的假设类和先验分布，使得两个仅在不可忽略质量点上不同的目标假设产生相同的样本分布，从而推导出极小极大下界。该构造统一适用于VC维和Littlestone维的情形。上界方面：针对d=1的情形，设计一种基于留一法(leave-one-out)分析的非恰当(improper)学习器，其分析借助单包含图(one-inclusion graph)的论证。

Result: 在VC维d≥2且插入预算有限的最坏情况下，极小极大期望误差为Θ((d/n)log(n/d))；在d=1时为Θ(1/n)。以Littlestone维d_L替代d时，同样的速率成立，这意味着干净的在线到批量(online-to-batch)速率O(d_L/n)不可达。添加正确标注样本反而使学习困难了一个对数因子，即使对具有有限错误界的在线学习类也是如此。

Conclusion: 不可交换性带来的额外对数代价是固有现象，而非特定算法的缺陷。这一反直觉的结果表明，即使所有样本都被正确标注，对手插入样本仍能通过破坏数据可交换性来实质性增加学习难度。

Abstract: A monotone adversary observes an i.i.d. labeled sample and appends a finite number of further examples of its choice, every one of them labeled correctly by the target hypothesis. The learner sees a uniform shuffle of the combined sample and is scored on the original distribution. Every example is correctly labeled, but the insertions depend on the clean sample, so the combined sample is not exchangeable. Larsen, Pabbaraju, and Shetty, who introduced this model, showed that empirical risk minimization attains expected error $O((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for classes of VC dimension $d$, and that every known optimal learner can be pushed away from the $Θ(d/n)$ rate, optimal for PAC learning. They asked whether the extra logarithm is an artifact of those particular algorithms or an inherent consequence of the lack of exchangeability.
  We show that this additional cost is inherent beyond VC dimension one. In the worst case over classes of VC dimension $d$ and over known finite insertion budgets, the minimax expected error is $Θ(1/n)$ at $d=1$ and $Θ((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for $d\geq 2$. The same rates hold with Littlestone dimension $d_{\mathrm L}$ in place of $d$, so the clean online-to-batch rate $O(d_{\mathrm L}/n)$ is unattainable as well. Thus, somewhat counterintuitively, adding correctly labeled examples can make learning harder by a logarithmic factor, even for classes that admit finite mistake bounds in online learning. The dimension-one upper bound is achieved by a simple improper learner whose analysis adapts the leave-one-out argument underlying the one-inclusion graph. All of our lower bounds are elementary and come from a single construction: an explicit class and prior on which two target hypothesis, which differ a point of nonnegligible mass, produce the same sample.

</details>


### [254] [Scalable estimation of VARMA models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06340)
*Daniel Paulin,Victor Elvira*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的VARMA模型估计框架，通过偏自相关重参数化、高斯先验和基于Parseval恒等式的固定大小充分统计量，使每次迭代的计算成本与序列长度T无关，从而将VARMA的可扩展性提升到此前仅VAR可达的维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VARMA模型的移动平均项能用少量参数捕捉纯自回归需要许多滞后项才能匹配的动态结构，但传统估计面临三大障碍：似然函数非凸、参数化仅在同构等价类下可识别、每次似然评估需要遍历整个序列。这导致VARMA在中等维度以上被认为不实用，实践者长期依赖VAR模型。

Method: 提出一个三管齐下的估计框架：(1) 偏自相关重参数化，通过构造保证平稳性和可逆性；(2) 对重参数化系数施加高斯先验，对角和非对角元素使用不同尺度；(3) 利用Parseval（Fourier）恒等式，以近线性截断代价将数据压缩为固定大小的充分统计量，使损失函数与序列长度T解耦。由此得到两个点估计器：正则化最小二乘拟合和协方差边缘化的最大后验估计。该框架以相同主导成本扩展至季节性动态、外生回归变量（VARMAX）和滚动窗口重拟合。

Result: 理论证明两个估计器在固定维度下以近参数速率恢复真实过程的无限自回归表示，截断不引入渐近偏差。在d=10到d=40的实证中，估计器的预测误差接近Oracle水平，而经典条件MLE返回不可逆拟合且预测发散。在零售需求、气象和空气质量数据上，该方法匹配或优于VAR、Bayesian-VAR、分量ARMA和稀疏VARMA基线。

Conclusion: 该框架使基于似然的VARMA估计以与序列长度无关的每次迭代成本，达到了实践者此前只能依赖VAR模型的问题规模，消除了VARMA在实际应用中的计算障碍。

Abstract: Vector autoregressive moving-average (VARMA) models have long been considered impractical beyond moderate dimensions: the likelihood is non-convex, the parametrization is identified only up to equivalence, and every evaluation costs a pass over the entire series. Yet their moving-average term captures with a few parameters what a pure autoregression matches only with many lags. We introduce an estimation framework that removes this computational barrier: each optimization iteration is independent of the series length $T$. The framework combines a partial-autocorrelation reparametrization that guarantees stationarity and invertibility by construction, Gaussian priors on the reparametrized coefficients with separate scales for diagonal and off-diagonal entries, and losses that depend on the data only through fixed-size sufficient statistics, evaluated by a Parseval (Fourier) identity at near-linear cost in the truncation length. This yields two point estimators: a regularized least-squares fit and a covariance-marginalized maximum-a-posteriori estimator. We prove that both recover the infinite-autoregressive representation of the true process at a near-parametric rate in fixed dimension, so the truncation introduces no asymptotic bias. The same machinery extends, at the same leading cost, to seasonal dynamics, exogenous regressors (VARMAX), and rolling-window refits. Empirically, the estimators stay close to the oracle forecast error from $d=10$ to $d=40$ (where classical conditional MLE returns non-invertible fits whose forecasts diverge) and match or beat VAR, Bayesian-VAR, component-wise ARMA, and sparse-VARMA baselines on retail-demand, meteorological, and air-quality data. This brings likelihood-based VARMA estimation, at a per-iteration cost independent of the series length, to the problem sizes where practitioners have so far relied on VAR models.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [255] [Agentic Nesting: A New Methodology for Existing Enterprise Application Integration and Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05159)
*Xi Wang,Kun Li,Xianyao Ling,Gang Yin,Liang Zhang,Jiang Wu,Wenbo Lei,Jun Xu,Annie Wang,Fu Zhang,Weizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agentic Nesting，一个将遗留企业应用封装为自主AI代理的多代理协作框架，通过"应用即代理"范式和"对话即集成"理念，实现跨异构系统的自然语言交互与流程编排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业依赖大量异构业务系统导致数据孤岛与流程碎片化，传统集成方案（ESB、API网关、RPA）存在架构耦合度高、运维成本攀升、智能化不足等固有局限，亟需一种更灵活、智能的应用集成方法。

Method: 提出 Agentic Nesting 多代理协作框架，包含三个核心设计：1）从每个遗留应用中提取数字代理代理，支持自然语言交互与自主操作；2）通过中央编排器协调多代理，实现任务分解与动态调度；3）暴露统一会话接口用于跨应用查询与流程编排。代理采用分层嵌套的管家拓扑（stewardship topologies）组织，映射企业生态的组成复杂性。

Result: 论文贡献了"应用即代理"（Application-as-Agent）集成范式和"对话即集成"（Conversation-as-Integration）交互哲学，并探讨了该方法在异构系统协调与大规模数据应用场景中的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: Agentic Nesting 框架通过将企业应用提升为可对话的自主代理并以层级嵌套结构组织，为克服传统集成方案的局限性提供了新路径，其核心范式具有跨场景推广价值。

Abstract: Enterprise operations extensively rely on multiple heterogeneous business systems and information applications, which also result in severe data silos and process fragmentation. Enterprises have invested considerable financial and material resources in building these applications, however, effectively leveraging and orchestrating them remains a formidable challenge. Conventional approaches to enterprise application integration, encompassing middleware architectures such as Enterprise Service Bus (ESB), API gateway infrastructures, and Robotic Process Automation (RPA), suffer from inherent limitations like high architectural coupling, escalating operation and maintenance costs, and limited intelligence capabilities. This paper proposes Agentic Nesting, a multi-agent collaboration framework in which existing enterprise applications are encapsulated as autonomous AI agents within a hierarchically nested structure. Rather than flat interconnection, agents are organized into layered stewardship topologies that mirror the compositional complexity of enterprise ecosystems. The framework extracts a digital agent proxy from each legacy application to enable natural-language interaction and autonomous manipulation, coordinates multiple agents through a central orchestrator for task decomposition and dynamic dispatching, and exposes a unified conversational interface for cross-application querying and process orchestration. The main contributions of this paper are the proposition of the "Application-as-Agent" integration paradigm and the "Conversation-as-Integration" interaction philosophy, together with an exploration of the generalization potential of this methodology in scenarios encompassing heterogeneous system coordination, and large-scale data applications.

</details>


### [256] [The Ignition Index: Measuring Global Workspace Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05160)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Ignition Index (I)，一个基于GWT的标量指标，通过拟合四参数sigmoid函数到逐层线性探针精度曲线，提取陡度参数β̂来量化Transformer语言模型中的"点燃"现象。在11个模型上验证，指标对真实语言结构的选择性是对照组的9.6倍。核心发现包括：前馈Transformer在点燃程度上显著优于SSM（89%差距）；循环架构沿迭代轴呈现更强的点燃特性；Pythia-410M在训练步数256处出现相变，早于induction head形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将全局工作空间理论（GWT）中"全或无点燃"这一核心动力学预测操作化为可量化的标量指标，从而在Transformer语言模型中建立GWT理论预测与机制可解释性之间的定量桥梁。此前缺乏经过验证的、可直接测量模型内部信息整合方式的指标。

Method: Ignition Index通过以下步骤构建：(1)对模型每一层训练线性探针（linear probe），测量其表征中蕴含的语言结构信息；(2)以输入信号强度为自变量、探针精度为因变量，拟合四参数sigmoid函数；(3)提取陡度参数β̂作为核心指标——高β̂值表示突变的、点燃式跃迁，低β̂值表示渐进式累积；(4)使用打乱标签的对照实验（shuffled-label controls）验证指标的选择性，采用Mann-Whitney U检验评估显著性。研究覆盖11个模型、5个架构家族，并引入PELT算法检测训练过程中的相变点。

Result: (1)前馈Transformer的聚合β̂值比SSM模型高89%（p<1e-13, Cohen's d=0.52），Mamba呈现近乎线性的表征累积曲线，表明其缺乏全局广播机制；(2)Huginn-3.5B沿迭代轴的点燃程度是沿深度轴的2.12倍，说明循环架构沿循环维度展现工作空间式跃迁；(3)Pythia-410M在训练步数256处被PELT算法检测到相变（β̂提升+67%），早于induction head的形成；(4)模型规模和信号强度与点燃程度的关系假设未被证实，提示Transformer架构可能已饱和其可用的点燃机制。

Conclusion: Ignition Index是首个经过严格验证的GWT动力学预测与机制可解释性之间的定量桥梁，具备9.6倍的测量选择性和架构级别的区分能力。研究揭示了不同架构在信息整合方式上的根本差异，为理解语言模型内部的信息处理动力学提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We introduce the Ignition Index (I), a validated scalar metric that operationalizes Global Workspace Theory's (GWT) all-or-none ignition prediction in transformer language models. The metric fits a four-parameter sigmoid to per-layer linear probe accuracy as a function of input signal strength, extracting steepness parameter beta-hat: high values indicate abrupt, ignition-like transitions; low values indicate graded build-up. Across 11 models spanning five architecture families, shuffled-label controls demonstrate 9.6-fold selectivity for genuine linguistic structure over spurious probe capacity (p < 0.001, Mann-Whitney U-test). We find: (1) Feedforward transformers exceed SSMs by 89% in aggregate beta-hat (p < 1e-13, Cohen's d = 0.52), with Mamba exhibiting near-linear profiles consistent with absent global broadcast. (2) Huginn-3.5B exhibits 2.12-fold higher ignition along its iteration axis than its depth axis, demonstrating that recurrent architectures manifest workspace-like transitions along the recurrence dimension. (3) Pythia-410M shows a PELT-detected phase transition at training step 256 (+67%), preceding induction-head formation. (4) Hypotheses linking ignition to model scale and signal strength were not confirmed, suggesting transformer architectures may saturate available ignition mechanisms. The Ignition Index provides the first validated quantitative bridge between GWT's dynamical predictions and mechanistic interpretability, with 9.6-fold measurement selectivity and architecture-level discriminability not previously characterized in the scaling literature. Code: https://github.com/saman-rahbar/ignition-index

</details>


### [257] [Woodpecker Distillation: Weak Models Diagnose Reasoning Bugs in Strong Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05168)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Yang Li,Deguo Xia,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大语言模型在推理任务上的失败常源于中间步骤的局部推理错误，而非全局能力不足。本文提出 Woodpecker Distillation，一种弱到强训练框架，通过对比局部干预来学习：在相同推理前缀后对比成功与失败的弱模型补丁，构建矫正性教师分布并蒸馏至强模型，在数学推理基准上持续提升强模型性能并超越直接模仿基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽具备解决推理任务的能力却频繁失败。作者认为这些失败主要源自中间步骤的局部推理错误（localized reasoning bugs），而非全局性能力缺失。关键洞察在于：这些错误是可修复的——在强模型推理前缀后插入弱探针模型生成的短补丁（patch）即可将推理轨迹重定向至正确解。然而，直接微调弱补丁或修复后的轨迹无法可靠地内化这一矫正效应，说明有效信号不在干预文本本身，而在于它如何重塑模型未来的推理分布。

Method: 提出 Woodpecker Distillation（啄木鸟蒸馏），一种弱到强的训练框架，核心思路是对比局部干预学习：① 在相同的推理前缀位置，对比弱模型生成的成功补丁与失败补丁；② 从这些补丁诱导出的未来 token 预测分布中构建矫正性教师分布（corrective teacher distribution）；③ 将该矫正信号蒸馏至强模型。该方法不直接模仿补丁文本，而是蒸馏补丁对后续推理分布的重塑效应。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，Woodpecker Distillation 能够持续提升强模型的性能，并优于直接模仿（direct imitation）基线方法。

Conclusion: 局部推理错误是大模型推理失败的重要原因且可被修复，但修复信号需通过推理分布重塑来传递。Woodpecker Distillation 通过对比局部干预蒸馏矫正分布，实现了弱模型辅助强模型推理能力的有效提升，为弱到强训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models often fail on reasoning tasks despite possessing the capability to solve them. We argue that many such failures arise from localized reasoning bugs in intermediate steps rather than from global incompetence. We show that these bugs are frequently repairable: inserting a short patch generated by a weak probe model after the same strong-model reasoning prefix can redirect the trajectory toward a correct solution.
  However, this corrective effect is not reliably internalized by directly fine-tuning on weak patches or repaired trajectories, suggesting that the useful signal lies not in the intervention text itself, but in how it reshapes the model's future reasoning distribution. We therefore propose Woodpecker Distillation, a weak-to-strong training framework that learns from contrastive local interventions. Our method contrasts successful and unsuccessful weak-model patches at the same prefix, constructs a corrective teacher distribution from their induced future token predictions, and distills this signal into the strong model.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Woodpecker Distillation consistently improves strong-model performance and outperforms direct imitation baselines.

</details>


### [258] [From Continuous Predictors to Clinical Thresholds: Early Evidence on Performance Trade-offs of Guideline-Based Categorisation for Ischaemic Stroke Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05203)
*Esra Zihni,Katryna Cisek,Hamzah Ziadeh,Hendrik Knoche,Robert Mikulik,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨将连续预测变量替换为符合临床指南的类别编码是否能在不牺牲性能的前提下提升模型可解释性。结果表明，在三个治疗队列中的两个，完全类别化模型与连续模型在预测准确性上无统计显著差异，全局特征重要性排序保持一致，证明基于指南的离散化是卒中预后模型的一种可行设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器学习模型在急性缺血性卒中90天预后预测中准确性很高，但临床采纳受限，原因是模型解释与临床医生的推理方式不一致。基于一项临床医生用户研究（该研究呼吁采用与临床指南一致的截断值），作者提出将连续预测变量替换为临床信息驱动的类别编码的设想。

Method: 在多中心欧洲注册数据上，按三种治疗队列分层，比较标准梯度提升模型与完全类别化梯度提升模型的性能。后者采用与卒中指南一致、治疗特异性的阈值对连续变量进行离散化。评估指标包括预测准确性及全局特征重要性排序的一致性。

Result: 在两个治疗队列中，完全类别化模型与连续模型在预测准确性上统计无显著差异；在一个队列中出现了显著下降。全局特征重要性排序在所有治疗组中保持一致，表明基于指南的离散化保留了预后因素的核心层次结构。

Conclusion: 基于临床指南的类别化编码是卒中预后预测模型中一种可行的设计选择，能在维持预测性能的同时，使模型解释更好地对齐临床实践指南，有望促进机器学习模型在临床中的实际应用。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve strong predictive accuracy for 90-day outcome prediction in acute ischaemic stroke, yet clinical adoption is limited by the misalignment of model explanations with clinicians' reasoning. Motivated by a clinician user study calling for clinical guideline-aligned cut-offs, we ask whether continuous predictors can be replaced by clinically informed categorical encodings without sacrificing performance. On a multi-centre European registry stratified into three treatment cohorts, we compare standard and fully categorised gradient-boosted models, the latter using stroke guideline-aligned, treatment-specific thresholds. The fully categorised models are statistically indistinguishable from their continuous counterparts in two of the treatment cohorts, with a significant drop in predictive accuracy in one cohort. Global feature importance rankings remain consistent, suggesting that discretising continuous predictors into guideline-based categories preserves the core hierarchy of prognostic factors across all treatment groups. Guideline-based categorisation is thus a viable design choice for stroke-outcome models.

</details>


### [259] [SkillTrace: Multi-Trace Provenance Auditing for LLM-Agent Skill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05204)
*Jialuo Chen,Minghe Wang,Lingqi Jiang,Jianan Ma,Xinhao Deng,Xiaohu Du,Ruixiao Lin,Yunhao Feng,Linkang Du,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SKILLTRACE 是一个面向 LLM-agent 技能复用的多痕迹溯源审计框架，通过提取表达痕迹、实现痕迹和操作痕迹（表示为技能操作图 SOG），在校准后确定性地比较缓存痕迹以判断复用关系。在基准测试中达到 AUROC 0.938、F1 0.898，并能在大规模审计中提供可操作的复用审查队列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-agent 生态系统中的可复用技能是包含元数据、自然语言指令、代码、工具、引用和操作流程的混合模态包。现有复用检测器仅针对单一模态源码或整体包相似度，无法捕捉仅保留技能某一部分的复用行为（如只复用文本描述或只复用代码实现），因此需要一种多痕迹溯源审计方法。

Method: SKILLTRACE 提取三种溯源痕迹：(1) Expression Trace（表达痕迹），(2) Implementation Trace（实现痕迹），(3) Operational Trace（操作痕迹），其中操作痕迹被建模为技能操作图（SOG），捕获激活、过程和资源流结构。LLM 仅在摄入阶段辅助操作痕迹的提取；审计时 SKILLTRACE 对缓存痕迹进行确定性比较，并使用同功能严格负样本校准各痕迹，最终报告哪条痕迹支持复用判定。

Result: 在 SKILLTRACE-BENCH 上，基于 100 个市场锚点技能、820 个变换后的正例和 751 个负例对照，SKILLTRACE 达到 AUROC 0.938、F1 0.898。在 36,446 个技能的野外审计中，痕迹归因证据能够识别出超越仓库级基线的可操作复用审查队列。

Conclusion: SKILLTRACE 通过多痕迹溯源有效解决了 LLM-agent 技能复用审计中跨模态、部分复用的检测难题，其痕迹归因机制不仅提高了检测精度，还为实际大规模审计提供了可解释、可操作的审查结果。

Abstract: LLM-agent ecosystems are rapidly growing around reusable skills: mixed-modality packages of metadata, natural-language instructions, code, tools, references, and operational workflows. As skills become marketplace artifacts, auditing their reuse is no longer the same problem as ordinary code clone detection. Existing detectors target single-modality source code or whole-package similarity, yet skill reuse evidence is distributed across authored text, implementation fragments, and operational structure. As a result, they can miss reuse that preserves only one part of a skill. We present SKILLTRACE, a multi-trace provenance auditing framework for LLM-agent skill reuse. SKILLTRACE extracts three provenance traces: Expression, Implementation, and Operational. It represents the Operational Trace as a Skill Operational Graph (SOG) that captures activation, procedure, and resource-flow structure. An LLM assists only the Operational-trace extraction, once at ingestion; at audit time SKILLTRACE compares cached traces deterministically, calibrates each trace against same-function strict negatives, and reports which trace supports a reuse decision. On SKILLTRACE-BENCH, with 820 transformed reuse positives over 100 marketplace anchors and 751 negative controls, SKILLTRACE achieves AUROC 0.938 and F1 0.898. A 36,446-skill wild audit further shows that trace-attributed evidence surfaces actionable reuse review queues beyond repository-level baselines.

</details>


### [260] [Abstract Event Causal Rules: Induction and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05205)
*Ziwei Zheng,Peiqiong Chen,Bang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对事件因果推理中低频长尾和未见事件组合泛化能力差的问题，本文提出抽象事件因果规则（AECR），通过多智能体具体到抽象因果归纳系统将实例级因果对转化为关系级抽象规则，并构建了基于规则引导的因果注意力编码器，在事件预测任务上显著提升了泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件中心智能分析系统依赖实例级因果对，但在低频长尾事件和未见事件组合上存在严重的泛化缺陷，亟需一种能从具体因果实例中提炼抽象因果逻辑的方法。

Method: 提出抽象事件因果规则（AECR）的关系级因果抽象范式；设计多智能体具体到抽象因果归纳（CACI）系统，结合相似性约束聚类从噪声数据中蒸馏可信AECR，构建两个完整知识库；提出抽象规则引导的因果注意力编码器（AR-GCAE），通过规则引导注意力层和门控表示融合将AECR注入因果图事件预测（CGEP）基准任务。

Result: 定量实验表明，引入AECR显著增强了事件因果推理的泛化能力，在事件预测任务上带来一致的性能提升，其中在稀有和未见事件样本上的增益最为显著。

Conclusion: 关系级抽象因果规则能有效弥补实例级因果知识的泛化短板，AECR作为一种可迁移的因果抽象范式，对提升事件预测系统的鲁棒性和覆盖面具有重要价值。

Abstract: Event-centric intelligent analytical systems heavily depend on explicit causal event knowledge for risk early warning, decision-making support and narrative comprehension. Nevertheless, existing instance-level causal pairs suffer severe generalization deficits on low-frequency long-tail and unseen event combinations. To address this limitation, this work proposes Abstract Event Causal Rule (AECR), a novel relation-level causal abstraction paradigm that transforms concrete cause-effect pairs into generalized abstract causal logic while retaining their intrinsic causal relationships. We design a multi-agent Concrete-to-Abstract Causal Induction (CACI) system coupled with similarity-constrained clustering to distill trustworthy AECRs from noisy raw causal data, based on which two complete AECR knowledge bases are built. To validate the practical utility of abstract causal knowledge, we propose an Abstract Rule-Guided Causal Attention Encoder (AR-GCAE), which injects the retrieved AECRs into the causality Graph Event Prediction (CGEP) benchmark task via rule-guided attention layers and gated representation fusion. Quantitative experimental results reveal that applying AECRs substantially strengthens the generalization capacity of event causal reasoning and brings consistent performance improvements to event prediction, with the most prominent gains observed on rare and unseen event samples.

</details>


### [261] [Otter: A Time-Aware, History-Conditioned Human Chess AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05206)
*Tarun Kumar S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Otter是一个1530万参数的国际象棋AI，通过将下棋建模为时间感知的序列过程来预测人类走法，结合了走棋历史编码器和时间控制模块，在610亿局面上训练，以55.23%的top-1准确率超越了Maia 2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人类象棋模型（如Maia 2）将每个局面独立处理，忽略了棋局的时间动态性——如开局偏好、局内行为漂移和时钟压力对决策的影响。作者认为将下棋建模为时间感知的序列活动可以更准确地预测人类走法。

Method: Otter结合两个条件信号：(1) 走棋历史编码器，基于最近20步棋的条件预测，捕捉开局偏好、局面漂移和局内行为倾向；(2) 时间控制模块，根据时钟压力调节预测。模型参数量为1530万，在1.17亿盘Lichess快棋对局（共61亿局面）上训练，单块T4 GPU训练30天。

Result: Otter达到55.23%的top-1和90.95%的top-5走棋预测准确率，超越了此前最先进的Maia 2模型，且参数量和训练数据更少。在11个Elo分段（<1100至≥2000）中，1900-1999分段准确率最高，达57.38%。

Conclusion: 将国际象棋建模为时间感知的序列活动，比仅基于局面的方法能更准确地预测人类走法，且模型规模更小。代码、模型和训练日志已公开。

Abstract: Otter is a 15.3M-parameter human chess AI that predicts human move selection by modeling play as a time-aware, sequential process rather than treating each position in isolation. It combines two conditioning signals: (1) a move history encoder that conditions predictions on the last 20 moves, capturing opening preferences, positional drift, and intra-game behavioral tendencies; and (2) a time control module that modulates predictions based on clock pressure. Otter is trained on 6.1 billion positions from 117 million Lichess rapid games over 30 days on a single T4 GPU.
  Otter achieves 55.23% top-1 and 90.95% top-5 move-prediction accuracy, surpassing the prior state-of-the-art human chess model, Maia 2, with far fewer parameters and less training data. Across 11 Elo brackets (<1100 to >=2000), accuracy peaks at 57.38% in the 1900-1999 bracket. These results show that modeling chess as a time-aware, sequential activity yields more human-accurate move prediction than position-only approaches, using a smaller model. Code, trained models, and complete training logs are publicly released.

</details>


### [262] [SearchAuditor: Auditing and Attributing Failures in Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05212)
*Zhixiang Liang,Yifei Liu,Yidan Huang,Haozhe Zhao,Beichen Huang,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan,Qiong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SearchAuditBench 是一个用于评估 LLM 审计者能否定位、归因和修复深度搜索代理失败轨迹的基准，包含 1,243 条失败轨迹。论文同时提出 SearchAuditor，一个多视角审计框架，在端到端通过率上达到 32.3%，优于 GPT-5.5 基线的 26.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索代理通过长程网页交互来回答复杂问题，但该过程复杂且脆弱：微小的推理错误可能在长而嘈杂的轨迹中传播，最终生成流利但错误的答案。诊断此类失败极为困难，需要人工检查极长的执行轨迹，可能超出人类能力范围。因此，需要自动化审计工具来减轻人工负担。

Method: 首先构建 SearchAuditBench 基准：从 8 个开放权重模型在 5 个深度搜索基准上收集 1,243 条失败轨迹（平均 73.1 条消息、65.1K tokens），并由专家标注关键错误步骤、搜索特定根因及参考修复方案与评分标准。其次提出 SearchAuditor 框架：采用多视角审计方法，通过基于证据的裁决来定位、归因和修复搜索代理失败。

Result: 最强基线（搭配 GPT-5.5 前沿模型）仅获得 26.6% 的端到端通过率；SearchAuditor 在不同前沿模型上均一致优于所有基线，端到端通过率达 32.3%，且其修复能帮助代理更好地从错误中恢复、继续失败的任务。

Conclusion: SearchAuditBench 为搜索代理失败诊断提供了标准化的评估平台，SearchAuditor 框架在多视角证据裁决下显著提升了审计效果，有效降低了人工诊断搜索代理失败的成本。

Abstract: Deep search agents tackle challenging questions through long-horizon web interactions, a process that is both complex and fragile: small reasoning errors may propagate through long, noisy trajectories into fluent but incorrect answers. Diagnosing such failures is difficult, requiring the manual inspection of extremely long execution traces, which could be beyond human capacity. We therefore introduce SearchAuditBench, a benchmark that evaluates whether LLM auditors can localize, attribute, and repair these failures, thereby reducing the human burden. SearchAuditBench comprises 1,243 failed trajectories, averaging 73.1 messages and 65.1K tokens, collected from eight open-weight models on five deep-search benchmarks, each expert-annotated with the critical error step, a search-specific root cause, and a reference repair with grading rubrics. We further propose SearchAuditor, a multi-perspective auditing framework that effectively localizes, attributes, and repairs search-agent failures through evidence-grounded adjudication. Experimental results show that even the strongest baseline, when powered by a frontier model like GPT-5.5, attains only a 26.6% end-to-end pass rate. In contrast, our SearchAuditor consistently outperforms all baselines across different frontier models, achieving an end-to-end pass rate of 32.3%, and resuming failed runs with its repairs enables agents to better recover from errors.

</details>


### [263] [PD-GS: Phoneme-Driven 3DGS for Audio-Driven Talking Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05218)
*Ao Fu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PD-GS通过在3D高斯泼溅说话人模型中引入时序对齐的音素token和语言融合模块，解决了唇部发音过度平滑和"漏嘴"伪影问题，在HDTF数据集上取得了最优唇形几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS说话人模型的唇部发音存在过度平滑和"漏嘴"（leaky mouth）伪影，根源在于从连续声学嵌入中以回归目标推断离散发音事件，导致预测偏向平均口型。自监督语音编码器虽提供丰富的韵律和语音线索，但缺乏帧对齐的显式语言目标来可靠消歧闭合级别的发音事件。

Method: 提出PD-GS框架：(1) 通过自动ASR和强制对齐管道获取时序对齐的音素token；(2) 核心组件语言融合模块（LFM）利用可学习门控机制自适应融合连续音频上下文与离散音素嵌入，在保持平滑音频驱动动态的同时增强发音关键片段的音素引导；(3) 仅使用单目视频以图像重建和唇部关键点监督进行训练。

Result: 在HDTF数据集上，PD-GS在唇形几何指标LMD上达到2.66，优于所有对比基线；定性上显著减少了挑战性音素序列中的闭合违规，生成了更具语言学忠实度的神经化身。

Conclusion: PD-GS通过显式引入时序对齐的音素信息有效缓解了3DGS说话人合成中的唇部发音不精确问题，证明了离散语言信号与连续声学特征融合在高质量神经化身渲染中的价值。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables fast, photorealistic talking-head rendering, yet accurate lip articulation remains elusive: mouth motion is often over-smoothed and may violate hard articulatory constraints such as bilabial closures, producing the notorious ``leaky mouth'' artifact. A key difficulty is that brief, discrete articulatory events are inferred from a continuous acoustic embedding under a regression objective, which biases predictions toward averaged mouth configurations. While modern self-supervised speech encoders provide rich prosodic and phonetic cues, they do not provide an explicit, frame-aligned linguistic target that reliably disambiguates closure-level events. We propose \textbf{Phoneme-Driven Gaussian Splatting (PD-GS)}, which augments a 3DGS talker with time-aligned phoneme tokens obtained from an automatic ASR and forced-alignment pipeline. Our core component, the \textbf{Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM)}, adaptively fuses continuous audio context with discrete phoneme embeddings through a learned gate, allowing the model to preserve smooth audio-driven dynamics while strengthening phoneme guidance on articulation-critical segments. PD-GS is trained purely from monocular video using image reconstruction and lip landmark supervision. On HDTF, PD-GS achieves the best lip geometry among the compared baselines (LMD 2.66) and qualitatively reduces closure violations in challenging phoneme sequences, yielding more linguistically faithful neural avatars.

</details>


### [264] [When Privileged Guidance Misaligns: State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05219)
*Junzhuo Liu,Weiwei Li,Jun Ling,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SMRC-SD提出状态匹配路由与上下文化自蒸馏方法，在多轮交互agent中仅在学生状态与参考轨迹匹配时才进行蒸馏，解决状态-参考不匹配问题，在ALFWorld和WebShop上显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮交互环境中，特权在策略蒸馏（privileged on-policy distillation）虽能提供逐轮的密集监督，但学生的动作会不断改变执行状态，导致其rollout可能进入参考轨迹未覆盖的状态，产生状态-参考不匹配（state-reference mismatch），使得参考信号不可靠。核心动机在于：提供与学生当前执行状态兼容的特权参考指导。

Method: 提出SMRC-SD（State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation）：(1) 在每一轮验证学生当前执行状态是否与参考轨迹上的某个受支持状态匹配，仅在匹配状态处施加蒸馏，过滤掉参考缺乏局部兼容指导的轮次；(2) 对每个匹配状态，从成功轨迹中构建状态条件化的教师上下文，将监督锚定在实际达到的状态上。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop两个benchmark上，SMRC-SD一致优于无条件的全路径蒸馏。使用Qwen3-1.7B，ALFWorld任务成功率从0.746提升至0.865，WebShop从0.574提升至0.693。受控的路由和上下文消融实验验证了选择局部支持轮次和构建状态兼容教师上下文两者均对性能提升有贡献。

Conclusion: SMRC-SD通过显式判定何时及如何使用特权轨迹指导在策略学生，有效解决了状态-参考不匹配问题。状态匹配路由和上下文化自蒸馏两个组件相互协同，显著提升了多轮agent的任务成功率，证明了在蒸馏中考虑状态兼容性的重要性。

Abstract: Privileged on-policy distillation provides dense supervision for multi-turn agents by allowing a synchronized teacher to re-score the student's response at every turn with access to training-only references, such as successful trajectories. In interactive environments, however, the student's preceding actions continually change the execution state. As the student takes different actions or completes subgoals in a different order, its rollout may reach states not covered by the reference, making the reference an unreliable source of guidance for the state actually reached. Applying privileged distillation indiscriminately therefore creates state--reference mismatch. This mismatch motivates a central objective: providing privileged reference guidance that remains compatible with the student's current execution state. We introduce State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation (SMRC-SD), which explicitly determines when and how a privileged trajectory should guide an on-policy student. At each turn, SMRC-SD verifies whether the student's current execution state matches a supported state along the reference trajectory. Distillation is applied only at matched states, filtering out turns for which the reference lacks locally compatible guidance. For each matched state, SMRC-SD further constructs state-conditioned teacher context from the successful trajectory, grounding supervision in the state actually reached. Across ALFWorld and WebShop, SMRC-SD consistently outperforms unconditional successful full-path distillation. With Qwen3-1.7B, it improves task success from $0.746$ to $0.865$ on ALFWorld and from $0.574$ to $0.693$ on WebShop. Controlled routing and context ablations support both selecting locally supported turns and constructing state-compatible teacher context as contributors to these gains. Code is available at https://github.com/liujunzhuo/SMRC-SD.

</details>


### [265] [Small Foundation Models of Human Cognition and Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05224)
*Nick Oh,Fernand Gobet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文训练了14个参数量从135M到14B的模型，在包含1070万试次选择的Psych-101数据集上进行认知微调，探究规模对认知建模的影响及模型是否习得任务结构。研究发现：分布内性能很快饱和，0.6B-1B参数即够用；但分布外泛化随规模显著提升。消融实验表明，刺激和反馈信息贡献了75.7%的习得信息，仅凭选择历史不足以解释模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型作为通用认知代理已崭露头角，但其所需规模以及模型究竟是在处理任务结构还是利用统计快捷方式，仍是悬而未决的问题。作者旨在系统探究规模定律在认知建模中的表现，并诊断模型实际依赖的信息源。

Method: 作者在Psych-101数据集（涵盖160个实验、1070万试次选择）上训练了14个模型，涵盖四种架构家族，参数量从135M到14B。通过分布内（留出被试）和分布外（新任务结构）评估考察规模效应。进一步通过两种诊断方法：①逐步剥离四个提示通道（任务指令、实验刺激、结果反馈、选择历史）共27个实验；②置换试次顺序，以揭示模型所利用的信息类型。

Result: 分布内评估中，规模几乎无关紧要，模型性能落在狭窄区间内，0.6B-1B参数即可匹配70B基线；分布外评估中，性能区间显著打开，呈现明显的规模梯度，大模型在新任务结构泛化上优势明显。消融实验显示，屏蔽刺激内容和反馈会破坏75.7%的习得信息并使模型降至随机水平以下，表明仅凭选择历史不足以驱动性能。置换实验发现，模型对独立试次任务具有不变性，但对试次顺序由先前响应决定的任务表现敏感。

Conclusion: 小规模认知微调模型有望作为心理实验的噪声上限估计器，但其适用范围受限于训练中见过的实验范式。规模仅在需要泛化到新任务结构时才变得关键。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned on human behavioural data have emerged as general-purpose cognitive proxies, but the scale this requires, and whether these models process task structure or exploit statistical shortcuts, remain open questions. We train fourteen models from 135M to 14B parameters across four architecture families on Psych-101, a dataset of 10.7 million trial-level choices from 160 experiments. In-distribution, scale barely matters. The models fall within a narrow band, as though against a ceiling, and 0.6B to 1B parameters suffice to match a 70B baseline on held-out participants. Out-of-distribution, that band opens into a markedly steeper scaling gradient, with larger models clearly advantaged in generalisation to novel task structure. To determine what information these models use, we run two diagnostics. We progressively strip four prompt channels -- task instructions, experimental stimuli, outcome feedback, and choice history -- across 27 experiments, and permute trial order. Masking the content of stimuli and feedback destroys 75.7% of learned information and pushes models below chance, demonstrating that choice history alone does not account for performance. Permutation reveals invariance on tasks with independent trials but sensitivity where trial order is determined by prior responses. Small cognitively fine-tuned models therefore show promise as noise ceiling estimators for psychological experiments, though their scope remains bounded by the paradigms seen in training.

</details>


### [266] [Project2Task: Graph-Guided Project-Level Planning for Autonomous Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05225)
*Huirui Xu,Runtao Xu,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Project2Task 是一种面向自主科研的图引导项目级规划层，通过"创新原子"和有向谱系图将项目分解为边界清晰、依赖明确的任务契约，显著提升了任务组合的连贯性与下游执行精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单任务科研智能体系统将整个项目视为单一巨型任务，或生成模糊、重叠的扁平任务集，缺乏对任务边界、并行替代方案和依赖顺序的显式建模，导致项目级科研自动化效果不佳。因此需要一种能够将长期科研议程分解为多个有界、可执行且依赖感知的任务的规划方法。

Method: 提出 Project2Task 层：(1) 将项目简介中的候选贡献表示为"创新原子"，并将其组织为有向谱系图；(2) 使用轻量级 Bernoulli 块模型目标函数，在水平、垂直和混合组合分解策略中进行选择；(3) 生成具有明确贡献归属的有界任务，修复重叠和缺失的执行字段；(4) 输出依赖感知的任务契约，包含目标、输入、预期产物、评估要求、边界约束、依赖关系和执行顺序。任务契约与具体下游执行器解耦，支持将任务输出整合为连贯的项目级结果。

Result: 在 10 个项目简介（约 30 个任务）的基准上，基于稿件的组合评估中 Project2Task 平均质量得分为 7.15，而 Brief Baseline 为 4.58，Topic-only Setting 为 5.31。将 Project2Task 契约与 AutoResearchClaw 集成后，下游任务平均准确率从 0.536 提升至 0.759。

Conclusion: 显式的项目到任务规划能有效生成连贯、无冗余且可执行的科研任务组合，Project2Task 的图引导分解策略和任务契约机制对此贡献显著。

Abstract: Research agents can increasingly search literature, propose hypotheses, generate code, run experiments, and draft manuscripts from a single topic. However, a research project is not merely a larger task: it is a long-horizon agenda that must be advanced through multiple bounded tasks with distinct but related objectives, parallel alternatives, and dependency-aware sequences. Existing single-task systems often treat the project as one oversized task, produce a flat set of vague or overlapping tasks, or leave task boundaries and execution order to manual coordination. We introduce Project2Task, a graph-guided project-level planning layer for autonomous research. Given a project brief, it represents candidate contributions as innovation atoms and organizes them in a directed lineage graph. A lightweight Bernoulli block-model objective selects among horizontal, vertical, and hybrid portfolio decompositions. Project2Task then generates bounded tasks with explicit contribution ownership, repairs overlaps and missing execution fields, and emits dependency-aware task contracts that specify objectives, inputs, expected artifacts, evaluation requirements, boundary constraints, dependencies, and execution order. The contracts are independent of any particular downstream research executor and support integration of task outputs into a coherent project-level result. On a benchmark of ten project briefs yielding roughly 30 tasks, manuscript-based portfolio evaluation gives Project2Task an average quality score of 7.15, compared with 4.58 for the Brief Baseline and 5.31 for the Topic-only Setting. Integrating its contracts with AutoResearchClaw increases average downstream task accuracy from 0.536 to 0.759. These results demonstrate the value of explicit project-to-task planning for producing coherent, non-redundant, and executable research-task portfolios.

</details>


### [267] [TriQua: Reconciling Granularity and Context in Factuality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05228)
*Jin Liu,Steffen Thoma,Achim Rettinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TriQua 提出一种基于三元组和超关系事实的自适应分解框架，通过为复杂声明附加上下文限定词来平衡原子性与上下文保留，并在验证阶段提供细粒度错误标注，其评分 TriQuaScore 与人类判断高度一致，在基于证据的事实验证上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 事实性评估的"分解-验证"范式存在根本性权衡：原子事实（一句一信息）常丢失关键上下文，而宽泛陈述又缺乏精确评估所需的粒度。需要一种能根据事实复杂度灵活建模、同时保留上下文和原子性的方案。

Method: 提出 TriQua 框架：(1) 简单声明提取为标准三元组（主-谓-宾）；(2) 复杂声明表示为超关系事实，即通过附加辅助上下文限定词（qualifiers）来扩展三元组，保留必要上下文；(3) 验证过程直接在三元组和限定词上标注具体错误，实现细粒度可解释性；(4) 提出 TriQuaScore 量化结构化事实单元的事实性得分。

Result: 实证评估显示：(1) TriQuaScore 与人类标注的事实性评分高度一致；(2) TriQua 实现了稳健的分解质量；(3) 在基于证据的事实验证任务上，TriQua 优于现有基于分解的框架。

Conclusion: TriQua 通过超关系事实建模成功解决了分解-验证范式中的上下文与粒度的权衡问题，其自适应结构和细粒度错误标注为 LLM 事实性评估提供了更准确、可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: The "decompose-then-verify" paradigm for LLM factuality evaluation faces a fundamental trade-off: atomic facts, i.e., one sentence conveying one unit of information, often omit essential context, while broader statements lack the granularity needed for precise assessment. To address this, we introduce TriQua, a framework that flexibly models facts based on their complexity. Simple claims are extracted as standard triples, while complex claims are represented as hyperrelational facts by attaching auxiliary contextual qualifiers. This adaptive structure preserves the necessary context for accurate retrieval and verification without sacrificing atomicity. Furthermore, TriQua's verification process directly annotates concrete errors within specific triples and qualifiers, providing fine-grained explainability for error detection. Alongside the framework, we propose TriQuaScore to quantify the factuality of these structured fact units. Empirical evaluations show that TriQuaScore strongly aligns with human annotated factuality scores, TriQua achieves robust decomposition quality, and outperforms existing decomposition-based frameworks in evidence-based fact verification.

</details>


### [268] [Coherence-Oriented Dream Scene Visualisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05233)
*Azra Açıl,Simon Colton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV)系统，该系统利用大语言模型将梦境文本描述拆分为四个时间片段，再通过文本到图像模型生成视觉连贯的四格图像序列，并对不匹配的图像进行重新生成。研究在DreamBank的50个梦境描述上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梦境具有强烈的情感体验，但在沟通和表达上存在困难。将梦境文本转化为可视化图像序列，有助于更直观地理解和分享梦境内容。

Method: DSV系统流程：首先使用大语言模型（LLM）对梦境描述进行提示引导，将其分割为四个按时间顺序排列的部分；然后使用文本到图像（T2I）模型为每个部分生成图像，并通过保持跨序列的视觉连贯性技术确保四格图像风格一致；对于与文本匹配度不足的图像，DSV会触发重新生成机制。评估采用CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL等视觉-语言模型，对50个DreamBank梦境可视化结果进行质量、保真度和连贯性三个维度的客观评测。

Result: 在DreamBank数据集的50个梦境可视化结果上，通过CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL等客观指标，报告了DSV系统在图像质量、文本-图像保真度以及序列连贯性方面的表现。

Conclusion: DSV系统展示了一种将书面梦境描述转化为视觉连贯的四格图像序列的有效方法，验证了结合大语言模型与文本到图像模型在梦境可视化任务上的可行性。

Abstract: Dreams can be emotionally intense but difficult to communicate. We describe the Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV) system which turns written dream descriptions into a temporal sequence of four panel images visualising the dream. This starts with a large language model prompted to split a dream description into four chronological parts. Then a text-to-image model produces images for each part with visual coherence maintained across the sequence, and DSV regenerates any image not suitably matching the text. We evaluate DSV over 50 visualisations from dream descriptions in DreamBank, and report quality, fidelity and coherence results via objective measures employing the CLIP, DINOv2 and Qwen2-VL vision-language models.

</details>


### [269] [Innovation-Residual Auditing of Autonomous Analysis Agents: Localization, Detection Limits, Error Control, and Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05490)
*Ahmed Hassoon,Mark Dredze*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Autonomous agents now carry out entire data analyses, selecting cohorts, joining tables, and fitting models with little step-by-step supervision. When such an analysis turns out to be wrong, someone must determine which operation caused it. A recent approach does this without any labelled mistakes, learning instead from analyses known to be sound and flagging operations that depart from what that model predicts; how reliable such audits are has not been studied. This paper supplies that analysis. The choice of score determines whether an error can be localized at all. If each operation is scored by how surprising it is given the operation immediately preceding it, then operations that merely inherit an earlier error are indistinguishable from correct ones, so one mistake produces one flag; scores computed against a longer reconstruction of the intended analysis instead spread a single mistake across many operations. We quantify how far they spread, and how to choose the comparison length when an error accumulates gradually rather than at once. We then give procedures that control the proportion of falsely flagged operations within a single audited analysis, requiring only that sound analyses be exchangeable rather than that the fitted model be correct, and we quantify how much the guarantees weaken when the model is imperfect or when the analysis was selected for review in a way that depends on its content. Finally we establish a limit on what any such audit can report: errors below a certain magnitude cannot be attributed at all, being indistinguishable from ordinary variation among sound analyses. This limit falls so slowly as more sound analyses are collected that at the representation sizes now in use a hundredfold increase reduces it by under two percent, so the dimension of the representation rather than the volume of training data is the binding constraint.

</details>


### [270] [Search2Skill: Skill Distillation Beyond Knowledge Boundaries Via Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05245)
*Muyang Ye,Tian Lan,Feihu Jiang,Yongshi Ye,Wuyunsiqin,Bin Zhu,Qianghuai Jia,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Ye Wang,Jinyang Zhang,Longyue Wang,Lingfeng Bao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Search2Skill 提出了一种新型自进化技能框架，通过自动识别智能体能力缺口、检索外部知识并将其蒸馏为结构化可复用技能，突破了现有方法仅依赖模型内部知识的局限。该方法采用基于评分标准的强化学习联合优化搜索时机、搜索方式和技能生成，在八个专家级领域的测试中持续超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化技能构建方法（如基于模型参数知识或轨迹的方法）受限于模型已知内容的边界。专业领域中的领域惯例和标准流程往往超出这一边界，且难以仅靠智能体自身激发出来。因此，需要一种能突破此限制、从外部获取并内化专业知识的方法。

Method: 提出 Search2Skill 框架，包含三个核心环节：(1) 自动识别智能体的能力缺口；(2) 检索外部资源以填补缺口；(3) 将检索到的证据蒸馏为结构化的可复用技能。框架通过基于评分标准（rubric-based）的强化学习进行联合优化，同时改进"何时搜索"、"如何搜索"和"如何生成技能"三个关键决策。

Result: 在三个基准测试、八个专家级领域上的实验表明，Search2Skill 在流式评估和留出评估两种协议下，均一致优于搜索增强基线方法和基于轨迹的技能学习基线方法。进一步分析表明，性能增益来自技能抽象本身而非原始检索证据，且所习得的技能可跨模型规模迁移。

Conclusion: Search2Skill 有效突破了模型内部知识的边界，通过外部搜索与技能蒸馏的结合，实现了 LLM 智能体在专家领域的自进化。其产出的结构化技能具有抽象性和可迁移性，为构建更强大的领域专家智能体提供了新路径。

Abstract: Reusable skills, which encapsulate the procedural knowledge required to solve real-world professional tasks, offer LLM-based agents a path toward self-evolution in expert domains. Existing self-evolving skill methods construct skills internally from the model's parametric knowledge or trajectories, and are therefore bounded by what the model already knows. However, the domain conventions and standard procedures underlying professional skills often lie beyond this boundary and are hard to elicit from the agent alone. To address this issue, we therefore propose a novel framework, Search2Skill, that automatically identifies the agent's capability gaps, searches external sources to address them, and distills the retrieved evidence into structured, reusable skills. Specifically, Search2Skill is optimized by a rubric-based reinforcement learning scheme that jointly improves when to search, how to search, and how to generate skills. Experiments on eight expert-level domains from three benchmarks show that Search2Skill consistently outperforms both search-augmented and trajectory-based skill-learning baselines under both streaming and held-out evaluation protocols. Further analyses show that the gains arise from skill abstraction rather than raw retrieved evidence, and that the acquired skills transfer across model scales.

</details>


### [271] [LUNAR: Benchmarking Personalized Large Language Models on UNiversal User BehAvioR Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05246)
*Jiahao Zhang,Yongzhi Tong,Zelin Fu,Pengde Zhao,Yanmei Jiang,Jiang Feng,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LUNAR 是首个基于纵向应用交互历史评估 LLM 跨领域行为个性化能力的基准，揭示了有效个性化依赖证据筛选与跨域整合，而非简单堆砌上下文或增大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化 LLM 基准依赖文本化人格或孤立行为信号，无法评估基于异构日常生活活动（衣、食、住、行）的跨领域行为个性化能力。

Method: 提出 LUNAR 基准，采用基于真实行为模式的多阶段由粗到细合成流水线构建数据，涵盖服饰、饮食、居住、出行等日常领域；并通过保真度分析与 19 个主流 LLM 实验，对比行为日志接入、细粒度检索与压缩记忆等策略。

Result: 行为日志是深度个性化的必要非充分条件；更多上下文或更大模型并不保证更好效果；细粒度行为记录直接检索一致优于压缩记忆；强个性化可能以隐私保护为代价。

Conclusion: 证据筛选、跨域整合与隐私控制是构建个性化 LLM 面临的三大核心挑战。

Abstract: Existing personalized LLM benchmarks primarily rely on textual personas or isolated behavioral signals, providing limited evaluation of cross-domain behavioral personalization, where responses must be grounded in heterogeneous daily-life activities. To address this gap, we introduce LUNAR, the first benchmark for evaluating how LLMs personalize responses from longitudinal app interaction histories across universal daily-life domains, including clothing, food, housing, and mobility. To support scalable benchmark construction while mitigating data sparsity and privacy concerns, LUNAR uses a multi-stage coarse-to-fine synthesis pipeline grounded in real-world behavioral patterns. Fidelity analyses show closer alignment with real behavioral distributions than other synthetic benchmarks. Experiments on 19 mainstream LLMs show that access to behavioral logs is necessary but not sufficient for deep personalization: neither more context nor larger models guarantees better performance; effective personalization depends on selecting and integrating relevant evidence across domains. Direct retrieval of fine-grained behavioral records consistently outperforms compressed memory, while stronger personalization can come at the cost of privacy protection. These findings identify evidence selection, cross-domain integration, and privacy control as key challenges for personalized LLMs.

</details>


### [272] [WorldClaw: Agentic 3D Open-World Generation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05248)
*Chunchao Guo,Jinpeng Li,Yang Li,Zilong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldClaw 是一个完全智能体的、由粗到细的框架，用于从开放式文本生成大规模、可自由探索的 3D 世界，同时保持全局空间一致性、丰富的局部内容和可编辑的实例级资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从开放式文本生成大规模、可自由探索的 3D 世界面临三大核心挑战：全局空间一致性、丰富的局部内容、以及适合下游编辑和复用的显式资产。现有方法难以同时满足这些需求。

Method: WorldClaw 采用完全智能体的由粗到细框架：(1) 规划智能体将文本提示转化为包含区域、地形、资产、材质和空间关系的结构化规范；(2) 基于语义布局、可复用资产、生成式或程序化材质以及区域感知高度场，构建全局一致的地形基础；(3) 对细节需求高的区域，生成以地形为条件的组合，重建可编辑的带纹理网格，并恢复其在地形上的放置位置；(4) 基于渲染的智能体进一步细化地形、物体、外观和接触关系。

Result: 在多样化的开放世界提示下，WorldClaw 成功生成大规模场景，具备一致的空间组织、视觉上引人入胜的局部内容以及可编辑的实例级资产，同时保持一致的全局地形结构。

Conclusion: WorldClaw 通过智能体驱动的由粗到细流水线，有效解决了开放世界 3D 场景生成中全局一致性与局部细节丰富性之间的平衡问题，为下游编辑和复用提供了显式资产支持。

Abstract: Generating large-scale, freely explorable 3D worlds from open-ended text remains challenging because a system must jointly maintain global spatial coherence, rich local content, and explicit assets suitable for downstream editing and reuse. We present WorldClaw, a fully agentic, coarse-to-fine framework for open-world 3D scene generation. Planning agents translate a text prompt into a structured specification of regions, terrain, assets, materials, and spatial relations. WorldClaw then builds a globally coherent terrain foundation from semantic layouts, reusable assets, generative or procedural materials, and a region-aware height field. For detail-demanding regions, it generates terrain-conditioned compositions, reconstructs editable textured meshes, and recovers their placement on the terrain; render-based agents further refine terrain, objects, appearance, and contacts. Across diverse open-world prompts, WorldClaw produces large-scale scenes with coherent spatial organization, visually compelling local content, and editable instance-level assets while preserving a consistent global terrain structure.

</details>


### [273] [Posture and Sustainment Optimization Under Adversarial Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05256)
*Amelie Norris,Alyssa Lee,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对联合作战规划中的兵力预部署问题，提出场景加权的对抗鲁棒态势优化方法。将问题建模为有限时域MDP，设计了CEV优化器及其鲁棒扩展RobustCEV，在印太想定实验中证明：贪婪基线因地理覆盖不足导致25.1%效率损失和57.3%战备崩溃；CEV可恢复19.8%效率；RobustCEV在面对欺骗性威胁先验时可恢复158%效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前联合作战规划中的兵力预部署（Pre-commitment posture）依赖贪婪启发式算法，仅追求价值最大化而忽视地理覆盖，在对手针对高价值目标实施打击时存在结构性脆弱。该问题在形式上尚未得到解决，亟需一种具备对抗鲁棒性的态势优化方法。

Method: 将兵力与可持续性分配（PSA）问题建模为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），涵盖资产、战区位置和时间步三个维度。提出两种优化器：（1）CEV优化器（Composite Expected Value），通过最大化场景加权期望态势效率，在威胁场景分布上进行资产配置；（2）RobustCEV扩展，引入贝叶斯对手模型，对手根据观测到的部署动态更新其目标打击分布，形成迭代对抗优化。实验在印太基地环境中以20项资产和5个战区位置进行验证，采用配对t检验（Bonferroni校正）和两层方差分解进行统计显著性验证。

Result: 三项实验结果表明：（1）贪婪基线因地理覆盖不足导致永久性25.1%的态势效率损失，并在价值相关的对抗威胁下出现57.3%的场景加权战备崩溃；（2）当威胁分布携带地理信号时，CEV优化器较贪婪基线可恢复高达19.8%的效率，且仅需5至20个精选场景即可捕获大部分收益；（3）当自适应对手采用欺骗性威胁先验时，RobustCEV相较于朴素优化器可恢复高达158%的效率。所有结果均通过统计检验，确认性能差距为部署策略的结构性属性而非采样伪影。

Conclusion: 本文提出的场景加权对抗鲁棒优化框架有效解决了兵力预部署中贪婪策略的结构性缺陷，CEV和RobustCEV在多种威胁条件下均展现出显著的效率恢复能力。有限数量的精选场景即可实现大部分优化收益，且统计检验证实了方法的可靠性，为联合作战规划中的态势优化提供了形式化解决方案。

Abstract: Pre-commitment posture, the assignment of military assets to theater locations before conflict scenarios resolve, is a critical and formally unsolved problem in joint operational planning. Current practice relies on greedy heuristics that maximize value and ignore geographic coverage and are structurally vulnerable to adversaries that target high-strategic value locations. This paper presents a scenario-weighted adversarially robust posture optimization engine for the Posture and sustainability allocation (PSA) problem, modeled as a finite-horizon Markov Decision Process over assets, theater locations, and time steps. We introduce the Composite Expected Value (CEV) optimizer, which places assets by maximizing scenario-weighted expected posture efficiency over a distribution of threat scenarios, and the RobustCEV extension, which iterates against a Bayesian adversary that updates its targeting distribution in response to observed placement. Across three experiments in an Indo-Pacific basing environment with 20 assets and 5 theater locations, we demonstrate that: (1) the greedy baseline incurs a permanent 25.1% posture efficiency penalty due to geographic under-coverage and a 57.3% scenario-weighted readiness collapse under value-correlated adversarial threat; (2) the CEV optimizer recovers up to 19.8% efficiency over greedy when the threat distribution carries a geographic signal, with a curated set of 5 to 20 scenarios sufficient to capture the majority of this gain; and (3) the RobustCEV extension recovers up to 158% efficiency relative to a naive optimizer when an adaptive adversary employs a deceptive threat prior. All findings are validated using paired t-tests with Bonferroni correction and two-level variance decomposition, confirming that the performance gaps reported are structural properties of placement strategies rather than sampling artifacts.

</details>


### [274] [OrchestraBench: Evaluating Multi-Agent Orchestration Failure Modes, Recovery, and Decomposition Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05263)
*Yidian Chen,Yingzi Gu,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty,Sharon Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OrchestraBench 是一个面向多智能体编排框架的评估基准，通过可控故障注入评估故障级联、恢复与任务分解能力，揭示不同路由策略和故障处理模式的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅报告任务准确率，未诊断流水线失败原因、级联起点或路由决策失误。生产环境中需要理解系统如何失败、如何恢复，而非仅关注最终得分。

Method: 构建种子可复现的故障注入测试平台，基于模板化企业工作流；引入级联半径（cascade radius）和按故障模式恢复率作为核心指标；对比关键词/标志路由与意图推理模型路由，使用 bootstrap 置信区间和配对检验；通过可验证算术依赖链上的受控机制探针，在五种 MAST 模式下测试 Claude 代理的故障处理层级；进行盲重试、可信状态修复消融实验，并测试不同模型规模（Sonnet/Opus/Haiku）及任务框架（算术链 vs. 贷款审批工作流）下的一致性。

Result: 关键词路由器在对抗性案例（误导或缺失表面标志）上准确率为 0%，意图推理模型路由器达 100%，匹配预言机。故障处理分为三档：工具故障完全恢复（1.0），模糊委托部分恢复（0.30），三种潜在/语义模式完全无法恢复（0.0）。该排序在贷款审批框架和三种模型间均保持稳定。盲重试复现潜在故障并增加检测时间。级联半径随流水线深度增加（深度 3-7 时均值从 0.9 升至 4.7）。可信状态修复消融表明，表面遏制效果主要来自可信状态信号而非自主检测。

Conclusion: 故障检测与归因对级联遏制至关重要；意图感知路由在对抗条件下显著优于表面标志路由；故障恢复能力存在清晰的分层结构，且在不同上下文和模型规模间保持稳健；当前自主检测能力有限，可信状态信号仍是恢复的关键因素。

Abstract: Multi-agent orchestration frameworks are moving from demos to production, yet benchmarks typically report task accuracy without diagnosing why a pipeline failed, where a cascade began, or which routing decision caused the breakdown. OrchestraBench evaluates failure, recovery, and decomposition through a controlled, seed-reproducible failure-injection harness over templated enterprise workflows. It introduces cascade radius and per-failure-mode recovery as primary metrics and compares routing policies with bootstrap confidence intervals and paired tests. On a 26-case gold-labelled diagnostic, a keyword/flag router scored 0% on adversarial cases with misleading or missing surface flags, whereas an intent-reasoning model router scored 100%, matching the oracle. Controlled mechanism probes with a real Claude agent over a verifiable arithmetic dependency chain revealed three failure-handling tiers across five MAST modes: tool faults recovered fully (1.0), ambiguous delegation recovered partially (0.30), and three latent or semantic modes never recovered (0.0). This ordering persisted when the computation was reframed as a loan-approval workflow and across Sonnet, Opus, and Haiku, although absolute rates shifted with context. Blind retry reproduced latent faults and increased time to detection, indicating that detection and attribution are necessary for containment. Cascade radius increased with pipeline depth (mean 0.9 to 4.7 across depths 3-7). A trusted-state repair ablation showed that apparent containment gains primarily came from the trusted-state signal rather than autonomous detection. These results are controlled-chain mechanism probes, not domain-workload claims.

</details>


### [275] [Agentic self-driving microscopy benchmarks support qualification but do not necessarily generalize to unseen tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05266)
*Nathan S Johnson,Ian Abshire*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个用于显微镜智能体控制器的基准测试与追踪日志框架，评估了不同智能体架构（拓扑、LLM选择、RAG参数等）在53项显微镜任务上的表现。结果表明，该基准适用于回归测试与直接比较，但无法可靠预测智能体在未见任务上的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体正越来越多地被用于控制显微镜、同步辐射光束线等科学表征工具，但该领域尚缺乏成熟的工程范式。研究者在设计显微镜智能体时面临众多架构选择，且不仅需要验证已知任务上的正确性，还需评估其对新任务的泛化能力，因此亟需一个系统化的评估框架。

Method: 开发了一个基准测试与追踪日志框架，系统评估了以下变量：三种智能体图拓扑（单智能体、双智能体、三智能体）、五种LLM、RAG与上下文参数、以及操作约束。共涵盖53项显微镜基准测试，记录了105种智能体配置、1,949次独立测试运行和49,109次RAG检索。进一步训练了代理模型（surrogate models），尝试根据架构与测试结果预测智能体在新任务上的表现。

Result: 不同配置之间在延迟、token消耗、成本和失效模式上存在显著差异。然而，基于智能体架构与已有测试结果训练的代理模型无法可靠预测智能体在未见任务上的性能。

Conclusion: 该基准测试框架适用于资格认证、回归测试、故障诊断和直接比较，但当前异构测试套件尚不支持构建任务无关的全局配置模型，即无法仅凭基准测试结果外推智能体在新任务上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly being developed to control a wide range of scientific characterization tools including microscopes and synchrotron beamlines. Research into agentic control of physical infrastructure is nascent and there are few well-established paradigms for how to engineer an agentic system. There are many choices to make when designing a microscopy agent, including the choice of LLM, the number of agents to use, agent responsibilities and delegation rules, retrieval-augmented generation parameters, and more. When designing and optimizing an agentic microscope controller, researchers not only want to ensure that the agent can correctly perform known tasks but also that the agent can generalize to new tasks that it has not encountered before. In this study, we develop a benchmark and trace-logging framework that reveals a) how different choices of agent architecture impact performance at microscopy tasks and b) the limitations of benchmarks for predicting if a particular agent will perform well on unseen microscopy tasks. The framework was used to evaluate one-, two-, and three-agent graph topologies, five LLMs, RAG and context parameters, and operational constraints across 53 microscopy benchmark tests. In total, 105 agent configurations, 1,949 individual test runs, and 49,109 RAG retrievals were recorded. Direct comparisons showed clear differences in latency, token use, cost, and failure mode between configurations. However, surrogate models trained on agent architecture and test results did not reliably predict an agent's performance on new, unseen tasks. These results show that these benchmarks are useful for qualification, regression testing, diagnosis, and direct comparison, but the current heterogeneous test suite does not support a task-independent global configuration model.

</details>


### [276] [CASCADE: An Agentic Regulatory Network Framework for Patient-Data-Validated Downstream Perturbation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05359)
*Jose A. Bird*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CASCADE是一个基于MCP接口的智能体框架，利用ARACNe调控网络预测基因扰动的下游转录效应。研究通过TCGA肿瘤数据的拷贝数扩增验证了其对MYC等基因敲低方向的预测准确性，并评估了LLM智能体将自然语言请求正确映射为工具调用的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基因扰动预测工具的验证通常仅检验预测基因是否属于已知癌症基因集合（成员资格验证），而缺乏对预测方向（上调/下调）是否与真实生物学一致的方向性验证。本文旨在填补这一空白，采用基于拷贝数扩增的剂量替代策略进行更严格的验证。

Method: (1) 利用ARACNe算法预计算基因调控网络，通过MCP协议暴露为工具；(2) 以目标基因拷贝数扩增作为敲低反向的剂量替代，在TCGA真实肿瘤数据中验证CASCADE预测的敲低靶基因表达变化方向；(3) 通过置换检验和PAM50亚型对照排除混淆；(4) 在METABRIC独立队列中进行复现；(5) 用Fisher精确检验与MSigDB基因集基线比较；(6) 扩展到15个额外基因检验通用性；(7) 另外对LLM智能体进行35个自然语言查询的MCP工具调用基准测试。

Result: MYC基因：在三个癌种中（BRCA: 90.0%, COAD: 72.0%, STAD: 85.7%, 均p<0.0013），预测敲低方向与实际扩增vs非扩增肿瘤表达高度一致，显著优于置换基线，并在METABRIC队列复现（87.2%）。但与MSigDB基因集基线相比，并未超越已有公开生物学知识。扩展到15个基因后，增殖机制调控因子大多复现成功，而谱系身份转录因子和CCND2持续失败。LLM基准测试中，本地模型工具调用精确匹配率71.4%，更大模型达85.7%；但两个模型在模糊查询上均自信地默认选择错误扰动类型，且针对性修复因触发条件从未出现而失败。

Conclusion: CASCADE在方向性预测验证方面相比传统成员资格验证更为严格，但其准确性未超越已有公共知识（如MSigDB），且验证成功具有基因特异性而非普适性。LLM智能体在明确查询上表现良好，但在歧义查询的扰动类型判断上存在系统性失败，提示人机交互的歧义消解是待解决的关键挑战。

Abstract: CASCADE is an agentic framework that predicts downstream transcriptional effects of gene perturbation from precomputed
  ARACNe regulatory networks, exposed via MCP. Prior work validates such tools by checking whether predicted genes are
  known cancer genes (membership); we instead test whether the predicted direction of change matches reality, using
  focal-gene copy-number amplification as a dosage-based proxy for the inverse of knockdown against real TCGA patient
  tumor data.
  For MYC, CASCADE's predicted knockdown targets show strong concordance with real amplified-vs-non-amplified tumor
  expression across three cancer types (BRCA: 90.0%, COAD: 72.0%, STAD: 85.7%; all p<0.0013), well above permutation
  baselines, surviving a PAM50 subtype control and replicating in an independent cohort (METABRIC, 87.2%). Compared
  against curated MSigDB gene-set baselines via Fisher's exact test, CASCADE's accuracy is not shown to exceed existing
  public knowledge of MYC- or E2F-driven biology, though its gene-specific direction-calling clearly outperforms a naive
  uniform guess.
  Extending to fifteen additional genes, validation proves gene-specific rather than universal: proliferation-machinery
  regulators mostly replicate, while lineage-identity transcription factors and one cyclin-D paralog (CCND2)
  consistently fail, a pattern we discuss as a hedged, post-hoc hypothesis.
  We separately benchmark whether an LLM-based agent correctly grounds natural-language requests into CASCADE's real MCP
  tool calls. Across 35 queries, a documented local model reaches 71.4% exact match (85.7% for a larger model); schema
  and gene-alias failures are resolved by scale or server-side correction, but both models confidently default to the
  wrong perturbation type on ambiguous queries, a failure a targeted fix could not resolve because its trigger condition
  never occurs.

</details>


### [277] [Counterfactual Analysis via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05367)
*Zonghao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究GPT-3.5在在线借贷场景中的反事实分析应用，发现提示工程可将GPT的R²从1.97%提升至2.84%，接近梯度提升回归的3.48%，并展示了LLM在生成反事实ROI时具备逻辑连贯性和因果推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实分析对决策至关重要，但传统方法依赖结构化数据和统计模型。作者希望探索大语言模型（LLM）在反事实预测中的潜力，尤其是在在线借贷领域评估不同利率方案的反事实投资回报率（ROI）。

Method: 以GPT-3.5为核心工具，首先通过提示工程优化预测性能，与梯度提升回归等先进机器学习算法进行基准对比（以R²为评估指标）；随后，利用GPT在给定一组替代利率条件下生成反事实ROI，并定性评估其逻辑连贯性和因果推理质量。

Result: 提示工程使GPT的R²从1.97%显著提升至2.84%，逼近梯度提升回归的3.48%；GPT在生成反事实ROI时表现出逻辑连贯性和因果推理能力。

Conclusion: LLM有潜力成为在线借贷及其他预测与决策场景中有效的反事实分析工具，提示工程是释放其能力的关键手段。

Abstract: Counterfactual analysis aims to predict potential outcomes under hypothetical scenarios, offering valuable insights for decision-making. This paper investigates the application of large language models (LLMs), specifically the GPT-3.5 model, for counterfactual analysis. We focus on the online lending context, where the counterfactual return on investment (ROI) is crucial for evaluating different interest rate schemes. We begin by assessing the predictive performance of GPT and comparing it with advanced machine learning algorithms. The results show that prompt engineering can significantly enhance GPT's predictions, with the R-squared increasing from 1.97% to 2.84%, closely approaching the 3.48% achieved by gradient-boosted regression. Subsequently, we utilize GPT to generate counterfactual ROIs under a set of alternative interest rates. GPT exhibits logical coherence and causal reasoning in its responses. The findings underscore the potential of LLMs as effective tools for counterfactual analysis in online lending, suggesting broader applications for LLMs in various predictive and decision-making contexts.

</details>


### [278] [DoctorAgents: an agentic framework to iteratively refine AutoML pipeline for small clinical temporal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05375)
*Ruilin Wang,Bo-Hong Wang,Elizabeth Kourbatski,Jun Bai,Hegang Chen,Ziyang Song,Gilles Boire,Marie Hudson,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DoctorAgents 将小样本临床数据的 AutoML 从暴力搜索重新定义为推理驱动的迭代优化，通过多个专用 LLM agent 协同构建、验证和精化 ML 流程，并利用文本梯度下降实现基于自然语言反馈的定向更新，在多种临床任务上一致优于现有 AutoML 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床 ML 面临数据稀缺、异质性强和时间复杂性高的挑战，现有 AutoML 系统依赖预定义空间的暴力搜索，缺乏显式推理与记忆能力，难以有效应对小样本临床数据场景。因此需将 AutoML 范式从穷举搜索转变为推理驱动的精化。

Method: 提出 DoctorAgents 框架：由多个专用 LLM agent（生成 agent、验证 agent、精化 agent）协同工作，自主构建和优化端到端 ML 流程。核心机制是通过「文本梯度下降」反向传播自然语言反馈，实现对流程的定向更新，避免暴力搜索。

Result: 在多种临床任务实验中，DoctorAgents 持续优于现有 AutoML 基线方法，并且生成的特定任务表征具有更好的可解释性。

Conclusion: 基于推理驱动精化的 agentic AI 框架能有效应对小样本临床数据的 AutoML 挑战，文本梯度下降机制为临床 ML 流程优化提供了高效且可解释的新路径。

Abstract: Clinical machine learning (ML) has the potential to support high-stakes medical decision-making, but reliable deployment is often constrained by scarce, heterogeneous, and temporal complexity. Developing effective ML pipelines for such data remains time-consuming and error-prone, while existing automated machine learning (AutoML) systems only partially address this challenge because they largely rely on brute-force search over predefined spaces and lack explicit reasoning and memory. We therefore reformulate AutoML for small clinical data from exhaustive search to reasoning-driven refinement. We propose DoctorAgents, an agentic AI framework that autonomously constructs and optimizes end-to-end ML pipelines through specialized large language model (LLM) agents for generation, validation, and refinement. DoctorAgents backpropagates natural-language feedback through textual gradient descent to perform targeted updates without exhaustive search. Experiments across diverse clinical tasks show that DoctorAgents consistently outperforms established AutoML baselines while producing more interpretable task-specific representations.

</details>


### [279] [C$^3$PO: Evaluating Cross-Modal Composition and Counterfactual Performance in Omnimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05381)
*Swapnanil Mukherjee,Agyeya Negi,Tanuja Ganu,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: C³PO是一个跨模态推理基准测试，包含3404个样本，涵盖视频、音频、图像和文本。研究发现即使是顶尖的多模态大模型也存在严重的模态偏见，86-95%的失败源于模型过度依赖单一模态（尤其是文本），揭示了多模态感知不等于鲁棒推理的关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型虽能处理多种感官输入，但其推理严重偏向主导模态（尤其是文本），导致跨模态推理脆弱不堪。缺乏针对性的基准来诊断跨模态推理失败的具体原因和时机。

Method: 构建C³PO基准（3404样本），采用信息组合（IC）和反事实冲突（CC）配对结构，四层难度设计，通过25个逻辑模板全自动生成。使用注意力探针分析模型内部机制，测量跨模态注意力分布与推理正确性的关系。

Result: 人类准确率88.64%，最佳模型Gemini-3.1-Pro仅73.17%，开源模型在冲突条件下崩溃。86-95%的失败源于模态主导：模型87-95%注意力集中在文本上，忽视矛盾证据。中间层注意力熵可预测正确性——持续探索成功，过早坍塌失败。相同复杂度的模板间存在56个百分点的准确率差距，表明性能取决于模态在冲突解决中的结构角色，而非模态组合本身。

Conclusion: 多模态感知并不保证鲁棒推理；模型架构必须具备维持跨模态注意力、避免过早偏向单一模态的能力，才能真正解决跨模态推理中的模态主导问题。

Abstract: Current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can process diverse sensory inputs, yet their reasoning remains heavily biased toward a dominant modality, resulting in brittle cross-modal reasoning. We introduce C$^3$PO, a benchmark of 3,404 samples spanning video, audio, image, and text, evaluating two abilities: information composition (fusing dispersed evidence) and counterfactual conflict (resolving deliberate contradictions). C$^3$PO's paired IC/CC structure and four-tier design enable targeted diagnosis of when and why cross-modal reasoning fails. Built through a fully automatic pipeline using 25 logically grounded templates, C$^3$PO reveals that while humans achieve 88.64% accuracy, the best model (Gemini-3.1-Pro) reaches only 73.17%, with open-source models collapsing under conflict. Through attention probes, we find 86-95% of failures stem from modality dominance: models commit to one modality while ignoring contradictory evidence, concentrating 87-95% of attention on text. Mid-layer attention entropy predicts correctness-sustained exploration succeeds, premature collapse fails. The 56-point accuracy gap between equally complex templates reveals that performance depends on modalities' structural roles in conflict resolution, not combinations. These findings show multimodal perception does not guarantee robust reasoning; architectures must enable sustained cross-modal attention to avoid premature

</details>


### [280] [Adaptive Arena-based Contestable Argumentative Network-of-Experts for Open-Ended Care Plan Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05391)
*Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Hoang-Loc Cao,Phuc Ho,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,René Richard,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CANOE 是一个多智能体神经符号框架，通过基于竞技场的量化双极论证框架（A-QBAF）实现可争议的护理计划协调，结合角色专业化智能体生成支持和反对论证，并支持人在回路的争议机制，在临床正确性、安全性和可解释性方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单一LLM流水线在护理计划协调中存在局限性：无法以透明、安全的方式综合来自多个专业学科的异构临床、功能和社会心理信息，缺乏可信的解释性和人类可争议性。

Method: 提出CANOE框架，包含五个模块：(1) 复杂度评估；(2) 自适应团队招募；(3) 基于A-QBAF的角色化论证计算——角色专业化智能体为候选干预措施生成支持和反对论证，通过竞技场式冲突解决机制消解矛盾，再在论证图上传播可接受性分数；(4) 人在回路争议——护理规划者可接受、拒绝、编辑或添加论证，框架确定性地重新计算最终计划；(5) 护理计划合成。

Result: 在Discharge Me!和MedicalRAG数据集上，使用ROUGE-L、AlignScore、MEDCON F1、FKGL和LLM-as-a-judge进行评估，结果表明医学微调模型在临床正确性和安全性方面表现最佳，而CANOE的论证结构提供了忠实的解释和人类可争议性。

Conclusion: CANOE通过神经符号多智能体论证框架，在护理计划协调任务中实现了透明度、安全性与人类可控性的统一，医学微调模型进一步提升了临床正确性，论证结构则保障了可解释性和可争议性。

Abstract: Care plan coordination demands synthesizing heterogeneous clinical, functional, and psychosocial information across multiple professional disciplines, where monolithic LLM pipelines cannot perform in a transparent or safe manner. We present CANOE (Contestable Argumentative Network-of-Experts), a multi-agent neuro-symbolic framework that addresses these limitations through five modules: complexity assessment, adaptive team recruitment, role-based argumentative computation via an Arena-based Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Framework (A-QBAF), human-in-the-loop contestation, and care-plan synthesis. Role-specialized agents generate supporting and attacking arguments for candidate interventions; conflicts are resolved through arena-based clash resolution before acceptability scores propagate across the argumentation graph. Care planners may accept, reject, edit, or add arguments, and the framework will deterministically recompute the final plan. Evaluation on Discharge Me! and MedicalRAG using ROUGE-L, AlignScore, MEDCON F1, FKGL, and LLM-as-a-judge shows that medically fine-tuned models achieve the strongest clinical correctness and safety, while CANOE's argumentative structure provides faithful explanation and human contestability.

</details>


### [281] [Evaluating and Improving Pedagogical Fit in LLM-Based AI Tutors with the Pedagogical Suitability Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05411)
*Benjamin Barlog,Hudson Craig,Zedong Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出教学适宜性指数（PSI），一个包含六个理论导向子评分的复合指标，用于评估大语言模型作为AI导师时生成回复与学习者准备状态和课程进度的对齐程度。实验评估了四种LLM在240个场景中的表现，并证明PSI引导的再生协议可显著改善弱表现案例（82.3%改善率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM作为AI导师的评估主要关注答案质量（正确性），忽略了教学适宜性——即回复是否匹配学习者当前基础、课程顺序和概念引入时机。课堂学习中的有效帮助不仅需要正确，还需要"教得合适"，这一维度缺乏系统性度量工具。

Method: 提出教学适宜性指数（PSI），由六个基于学习理论的子评分构成，用于评估LLM辅导回复与学习者准备状态和课程进度的对齐程度。实验设计：选取四种LLM（ChatGPT、Gemini、Gemma4、Qwen3），在240个场景中使用配对的标准提示和缺陷提示进行评估；对62个弱表现案例应用PSI引导的再生协议进行改进，并通过人工评估验证。

Result: 四种模型基线PSI得分差异不大（范围0.557–0.638），开源与闭源模型在教学适宜性上无显著差距。提示扰动下整体PSI基本稳定（Δ=−0.002），但子评分之间存在权衡。PSI引导反馈显著改善弱表现案例：62例中51例改善（82.3%）。人工评估验证了PSI识别弱点的教学意义及再生回复的改善效果。

Conclusion: 以学习者和课程为导向的对齐可能比模型类别本身对有效辅导更为关键，且这种对齐既可度量也可改进。PSI为AI教育应用的评估和改进提供了超越单纯答案正确性的新视角和实用工具。
