We introduce EnvACE, an agentic reinforcement learning method that replaces external environment interaction during training with world rehearsal. The policy alternates between acting and rehearsal: it first generates a tool call, then plays the role of the environment to produce the response induced by that action, and conditions subsequent decisions on the rehearsed response. Both roles are jointly optimized end-to-end using task-success rewards. Through world rehearsal, the policy internalizes the relationship between actions and their environment responses in its parameters, yielding an agent world model that directly supports decision making. Across BFCL-v4, tau^2-Bench, VitaBench, and FinMCP-Bench, EnvACE achieves strong and transferable performance, outperforming environment-scaling baselines in the overall evaluation. Controlled studies further show that world rehearsal consistently improves policy learning across model scales. At test time, the internalized world model enables private rehearsal before committed execution, yielding further gains under a moderate rehearsal budget without additional external interaction. Our findings establish world rehearsal as a new path toward scaling LLM agent training beyond the constraints of external environments. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Within-yao/EnvACE.

</details>


### [347] [TS-RAG: Retrieval Augmented Generation for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06223)
*Yixiong Xiao,Congxi Xiao,Jingbo Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TS-RAG 将检索增强生成引入时间序列预测，通过设计专用参考令牌融合输入序列与检索到的相似序列信息，在多个基准上取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 在 NLP 领域已证明有效，但在时间序列预测中应用有限。现有时间序列模型参数量小、训练数据少、缺乏大语言模型的生成能力，简单拼接参考序列（如 LLM 的做法）效果不佳，因此需要专门设计适合时间序列的 RAG 融合机制。

Method: 提出 TS-RAG 框架：首先检索与输入序列相似的历史时间序列作为参考，然后通过专门设计的 reference tokens 将输入序列信息与检索到的相似序列信息进行有效融合，从而更鲁棒地捕捉复杂时序动态模式。

Result: TS-RAG 在多个真实世界时间序列预测基准上持续取得 state-of-the-art 性能。

Conclusion: 将 RAG 思想适配到时间序列预测领域是可行且有效的，关键在于设计适合时序数据的融合机制（reference tokens），而非简单沿用 NLP 的拼接策略。

Abstract: While deep learning models, particularly transformer-based architectures, have shown impressive performance in time series forecasting, the application of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in this domain remains limited. Since RAG has proven effective in enhancing the capabilities of large language models by incorporating relevant external information, retrieving similar time series sequences as references might also improve accuracy in time series forecasting tasks. However, most time series models are constrained by limited training data, smaller parameter scales, and a lack of the extensive generative capabilities found in large language models. Simply concatenating reference sequences into the prompt, as done in language models, may not yield the expected results. To address these challenges, we propose a novel approach, TS-RAG, which leverages RAG to enhance forecasting performance. The framework introduces specially designed reference tokens to effectively fuse information from the input sequence with that from retrieved similar sequences, enabling a more robust capture of complex temporal dynamics. Experimental results demonstrate that TS-RAG achieves consistent state-of-the-art performance across several real-world forecasting benchmarks.

</details>


### [348] [DASH: Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons for On-Policy Self-Distillation of Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06243)
*ZhiYan Hou,Xinyu Tang,Hongyan An,Jianjin Zhang,Weizhen Wang,Yunyun Han,Gengsheng Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Haiyun Guo,Wenbin Hu,Jinqiao Wang,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DASH提出了一种基于发散自适应监督范围的方法，通过将局部蒸馏信号与序列均值之间的差距映射为自适应传播门控，控制反向多步聚合，从而根据局部发散的时间演化动态调整token级监督权重，弥补了标准OPSD无法利用rollout时间结构的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD)虽然通过密集token级分布监督缓解了RLVR中奖励信号的稀疏性问题，但它对所有局部发散赋予相同权重，忽略了发散发生的位置及其在序列中的时间上下文——相同的发散幅度可能对应不同的发散历史，反映师生模型间不匹配的不同演化过程。仅凭局部标量无法区分这些时间上下文，导致OPSD无法自适应调整token级权重。

Method: 提出Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons (DASH)方法：将每个局部蒸馏信号与序列级均值之间的差距映射为一个自适应传播门控（adaptive propagation gate），利用这些门控来控制反向多步聚合（backward multi-step aggregation），从而根据局部发散在生成过程中的演化方式自适应地调整token级监督权重。该方法复用了OPSD已计算的师生分布，无需额外的正向传播。

Result: 在三种模型规模、三个数学推理基准上的实验表明，DASH在所有基准和所有规模上均优于重新实现的匹配版vanilla OPSD基线。

Conclusion: 通过引入时间结构感知的自适应监督范围机制，DASH有效弥补了标准OPSD的不足，在不增加额外计算开销的前提下实现了一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models using automatically verifiable outcome signals, but these signals are typically sparse and at the sequence-level. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) mitigates this sparsity by querying a privileged teacher at student-visited prefixes and providing dense token-level distributional supervision. Although this dense supervision alleviates signal sparsity, we find that standard OPSD still underexploits the temporal structure of the rollout. It assigns every local divergence the same coefficient, regardless of its position or the divergence sequence in which it occurs. In on-policy autoregressive generation, the same divergence magnitude can follow different discrepancy histories, reflecting different evolutions of the mismatch between the teacher and student. Since the local scalar alone cannot distinguish these temporal contexts, standard OPSD cannot adapt its token-level weights to the realized discrepancy sequence. To address this limitation, we propose Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons (DASH). DASH maps the gap between each local distillation signal and the sequence-level mean to an adaptive propagation gate and then uses these gates to control backward multi-step aggregation. By doing so, DASH adjusts token-level supervision weights according to how local divergences evolve during generation. Experiments on three mathematical reasoning benchmarks across three model scales show that DASH improves over our matched vanilla OPSD reruns on every benchmark at all three scales. DASH reuses the teacher and student distributions that OPSD already computes, so the gains require no additional teacher or student forward pass.
  Code: https://github.com/DBtxy/DASH-OPSD

</details>


### [349] [Improving the Realism of Synthetic Clinical Benchmarks Under Utility Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06265)
*Omid Bazgir,Md Nasir,Jacob Hoffman,Yang Yang,Manu Agrawal,Anusua Trivedi,Vinay Rao Dandin,Chris Gibbons,Christine Swisher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在保持现有下游效用检查的前提下，提升合成临床基准测试的真实性。作者将基准修订形式化为"效用约束下的真实性改进"问题，以缺失结构、简洁性、结构合理性和人群对齐度衡量真实性。基线基准极为稀疏，两种确定性修订方法在维持效用底线的基础上改善了这些指标，而简单稠密化则保留了不切实际的模板化特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成临床基准测试即使通过现有效用检查，仍可能在结构上不真实，尤其在隐私敏感的医疗场景中运营数据难以获取。现有实践仅以效用检查作为真实性的充分证据，但效用与真实性是相关但不同的目标，需要显式优化基准真实性。

Method: （1）将基准修订形式化为"效用约束下的真实性改进"问题；（2）基于Synthea生成的患者数据，通过演示电子健康记录工作流构建护理差距基准，经与运营数据相同的下游流水线处理；（3）采用四个真实性维度：缺失结构、简洁性、结构合理性和人群对齐度；（4）设计两种确定性修订方法与一种朴素稠密化对照进行比较。

Result: 基线基准极为稀疏：采样对缺失率79.44%、仅12.75%行可执行、38.94%患者零可执行指标、前三token集中度达100%。两种确定性修订在保持当前效用底线以上同时改进了所有真实性面板指标；朴素稠密化虽通过效用检查但保留了不真实模板。内部基准真实性与对聚合运营参考的源保真度是相关但不同的目标。

Conclusion: 合成基准质量应被显式优化，效用仅应作为一项约束条件，而非真实性的充分证据。基准修订可以在不破坏下游效用检查的前提下显著提升结构真实性。

Abstract: Synthetic clinical benchmarks for enterprise AI agents can pass existing utility checks and still remain structurally unrealistic, especially in privacy-sensitive healthcare settings where operational data are hard to access. We study how to improve such benchmarks without breaking the downstream utility checks already used in practice.
  We formulate benchmark revision as utility-constrained realism improvement: dataset changes should increase realism while staying above an operational utility floor. We instantiate this idea on a care-gap benchmark derived from Synthea-generated patients exercised through demonstration electronic health record workflows and then processed by the same downstream pipeline as operational data. Realism is measured through missingness structure, simplicity, structural plausibility, and population alignment.
  The baseline benchmark is extremely thin: sampled-pair missingness is 79.44%, only 12.75% of rows are actionable, 38.94% of patients have zero actionable measures, and top-three token concentration reaches 100.0%. Two deterministic revisions improve these panels while remaining above the current utility floor, whereas a naive densification control preserves unrealistic templating. We further show that internal benchmark realism and source fidelity to an aggregate operational reference are related but distinct objectives. These results suggest that synthetic benchmark quality should be optimized explicitly, with utility treated as one constraint rather than as sufficient evidence of realism.

</details>


### [350] [The Illusion of Visual Tool-Use: A Causal Audit of Thinking with Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06270)
*Zhiheng Wang,Bo Peng,Lai Wei,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过因果推断框架审计多模态LLM中视觉工具使用（如裁剪缩放）的真实有效性，发现尽管聚合准确率有所提升，但视觉工具使用在大多数rollout中并不具备因果效力，揭示了"视觉工具使用的幻觉"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前"以图思考"范式给多模态LLM配备主动视觉操作，但实际增益微弱甚至为负，且存在重复裁剪无关区域、答错直接推理能答对的问题等现象。作者旨在探究返回的视觉证据是否真正因果性地影响模型答案。

Method: 将视觉工具使用建模为因果图，分离"观察中介路径"与"动作诱导捷径"。设计三个干预层级的审计框架：(1) 策略级——比较工具使用与直接推理；(2) 轨迹级——在rollout过程中破坏所有观察；(3) 步骤级——在固定前缀下反事实替换单个观察。核心估计量"视觉证据增益(VEG)"隔离每个返回观察的因果贡献。

Result: 在6个代表性模型和5个细粒度感知基准上，发现策略误校准的两种失败模式："只看不调用"(Calling Without Looking)——返回观察对答案无因果影响；"调用不规划"(Looking Without Planning)——观察有信息量但调用调度不连贯。轨迹级诊断显示准确率增益集中在少数"校准型"样本中。

Conclusion: 尽管聚合准确率有所提升，视觉工具使用在广泛rollout中并不具备因果效力，这一矛盾被命名为"视觉工具使用的幻觉"。该发现对多模态LLM工具使用范式的设计和评估具有重要启示。

Abstract: The "thinking-with-images" paradigm equips multimodal LLMs with active visual operations such as crop-and-zoom. However, models using these operations often achieve only marginal or negative gains over direct inference at substantially higher token cost. They may also repeatedly crop irrelevant regions and fail on questions that direct inference answers correctly. We ask whether the returned visual evidence causally affects the answer. To answer this question, we formulate visual tool-use as a causal graph that separates observation-mediated paths from action-induced shortcuts. We then audit it through interventions at the three levels: policy (comparing tool-use with direct inference), trajectory (corrupting all observations during rollout), and step (counterfactually replacing one individual observation under a fixed prefix). Our step-level estimand, Visual Evidence Gain, isolates the contribution of each returned observation. Across six representative models and five fine-grained perception benchmarks, we uncover policy miscalibration with two failure modes. In Calling Without Looking, returned observations have no causal effect on the answer. In Looking Without Planning, observations are informative but the call schedule is incoherent. A trajectory-level diagnostic decomposes the policy-level accuracy gain and shows that the gain is concentrated in a Calibrated minority. We term this discrepancy the illusion of visual tool-use: despite aggregate accuracy gains, visual tool-use is not causally effective across a broad range of rollouts. The code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/CauAudit.

</details>


### [351] [QuanTiMedAI: Quantum-Enhanced Time-Series Model guided by Agentic AI for Cardiac Arrest Mortality Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06294)
*Mutasim Fuad Sarker,Adiba Rahman Namira,Wafa Binte Alam,Md Adnan Arefeen,Mahzabeen Emu,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QuanTiMedAI 是一个量子-智能体混合框架，利用智能体LLM进行临床特征发现，结合紧凑的量子循环网络实现心搏骤停患者死亡预测。在MIMIC-IV队列上，仅用605个参数即达到AUROC 0.852，超越现有基线约2.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICU心搏骤停死亡预测研究多依赖入院早期的静态摘要数据，忽略了ICU住院期间患者生理状态恶化与恢复的时间动态演变过程，导致预测能力受限。

Method: 提出QuanTiMedAI框架：①智能体LLM（Agentic LLM）负责临床信息驱动的特征发现与选择；②紧凑型量子循环网络（quantum recurrent network）进行时序感知的死亡预测，通过非线性特征增强实现高效建模，参数量仅605个。在MIMIC-IV心搏骤停患者队列上进行广泛实验，并通过结构化消融实验验证各设计选择的贡献。

Result: QuanTiMedAI以仅605个参数达到AUROC 0.852，超越当前最优基线约2.9%；智能体LLM引导的特征选择一致优于传统方法；量子增强序列模型在参数远少于经典循环网络的情况下实现性能反超。

Conclusion: 量子增强序列建模能够在参数极少的前提下超越经典循环网络，结合智能体LLM的临床特征发现能力，为ICU时序预测任务提供了高效且可解释的新范式。

Abstract: Cardiac arrest remains one of the most lethal conditions encountered in intensive care units. Despite the growing availability of electronic health record data, existing mortality prediction studies in this population largely depend on static summaries derived from early admission. Such approaches ignore the temporal progression of physiological deterioration and recovery that unfolds throughout a patient's ICU stay. To address this limitation, we introduce QuanTiMedAI, a quantum-agentic framework developed for cardiac arrest mortality prediction using agentic AI guided quantum enhancement time series model. The proposed system combines an agentic large language model (LLM) for clinically informed feature discovery with a compact quantum recurrent network for temporality aware mortality prediction. Our findings demonstrate that agentic LLM-guided feature selection consistently outperforms conventional feature selection approaches, and the proposed quantum architecture achieves competitive predictive performance through nonlinear feature enhancement while keeping the number of parameters very low. Through extensive experimentation on a MIMIC-IV cohort of cardiac arrest patients, QuanTiMedAI's quantum-enhanced architecture attains an AUROC of 0.852 using only 605 parameters, an improvement of approximately 2.9\% over a current state-of-the-art baseline for this task. A structured ablation study systematically validates the contribution of each architectural design choice. These results show that quantum-enhanced sequential modeling can exceed classical recurrent networks while using substantially fewer parameters.

</details>


### [352] [Bias Analysis of L2 Speaking Assessment Systems Using Concept Activation Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06300)
*Arya Labroo,Mengjie Qian,Kate Knill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将概念激活向量（CAV）方法扩展到两类神经口语评估系统（BERT文本评分器和Whisper多模态评分器），用于检测偏见。同时探索稀疏自编码器（SAE）是否能提供更清晰的概念方向。研究发现：概念的可恢复性和敏感性高度依赖模型架构与表示层，SAE提升了线性可恢复性但削弱了原始激活空间的敏感性，表明偏见审计需区分"概念可恢复性"与"概念影响力"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动口语评估系统在高风险考试中广泛应用，但基于Transformer的基础模型作为"黑箱"，使得公平性和可解释性分析变得困难。先前工作使用CAV检测基于特征的评分器中的偏见，但尚未充分探索其在深度神经口语评估系统中的应用，也未解决复杂嵌入空间中线性可分性降低的问题。

Method: 1. 将CAV分析扩展到两个系统：基于BERT的纯文本评分器、基于Whisper的语音-文本多模态评分器；2. 使用CAV将人类可解释概念表示为激活空间中的方向，结合梯度敏感度度量区分"概念是否被编码"与"概念是否影响预测分数"；3. 引入稀疏自编码器（SAE），在稀疏潜在空间中学习CAV并映射回激活空间，以获取更清晰的概念方向。

Result: 1. 概念可恢复性强依赖于被探测的表示层和模型架构，而非概念本身；2. 概念敏感性同样具有架构依赖性；3. SAE使概念在潜在空间中更具线性可恢复性，但会削弱原始激活空间中的敏感度，尤其在低维层中表现明显。

Conclusion: 在审计口语评估系统的偏见时，必须区分"概念可恢复性"（概念是否被模型内部表示编码）和"概念影响力"（概念是否实际影响评分决策），二者并非等价。SAE虽然在概念提取上有优势，但其对敏感度的衰减效应需引起注意。

Abstract: Automatic speaking assessment systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings to mark second language (L2) learners' speaking tests, making it critical to show that their scores depend on speaking proficiency rather than irrelevant speaker attributes such as first language (L1) or age. Transformer-based foundation models have improved the accuracy of these L2 speaking graders, but their black-box representations make fairness and interpretability analysis more difficult. Building on prior work that used Concept Activation Vectors (CAVs) to detect bias towards unwanted attributes (`concepts') in feature-based graders, we extend CAV-based analysis to two neural speaking assessment systems: a text-based BERT grader and a speech-and-text multimodal grader based on Whisper. CAVs represent human-interpretable concepts as directions in a model's activation space, allowing us to distinguish between whether a concept is encoded in a model's internal representations and whether it influences the predicted score, the latter quantified using a gradient-based sensitivity metric. Since CAVs rely on linear separability, which is less likely in complex neural embedding spaces, we also investigate whether sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide cleaner concept directions by learning CAVs in a sparse latent space and mapping them back to activation space. Our analysis shows that concept recoverability depends strongly on the representation and architecture being probed, rather than on the concept alone. Sensitivity to concepts is also architecture-dependent. SAEs make concepts more linearly recoverable, but attenuate the original activation-space sensitivity, especially in low-dimensional layers. These findings highlight the need to distinguish concept recoverability from concept influence when auditing bias in speaking assessment systems.

</details>


### [353] [TRAJDEBUG: Tracing Error Lifecycle to Identify Critical Failures in Long-Horizon Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06346)
*Yunjia Qi,Zehua Yin,Xintong Shi,Hao Peng,Songyuanyi Lu,Yixian Liu,Richeng Xuan,Yuhong Liu,Zhichao Hu,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Bin Xu,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TrajDebug 提出了一种基于错误生命周期追踪的框架，通过多粒度历史压缩和证据驱动的错误识别来解决长轨迹中的关键错误定位问题，并构建了 TrajErrBench 基准以支持评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体系统在复杂任务中易出现级联错误且难以调试。现有关键错误检测面临两大挑战：（1）长轨迹中判断某步是否出错的证据分散在远距离的指令、观察和上下文中；（2）失败轨迹通常包含多个局部错误，但仅部分错误最终导致任务失败，需要区分其下游影响。

Method: 提出 TrajDebug 框架，采用错误生命周期追踪方法，包含两个核心机制：（1）多粒度历史压缩，将长轨迹信息浓缩以支持全局视角的错误发现；（2）基于证据的错误识别与归因，通过追踪每个错误的解决状态和最终影响，判定其是否为导致最终失败的关键错误。同时构建了 TrajErrBench 基准，包含来自 Tau2Bench 和 SWE-Bench Pro 的486条人工标注失败轨迹，覆盖工具使用和编程场景。

Result: 在多个智能体基准上的实验表明，TrajDebug 在关键错误检测任务上取得了相比现有基线方法最优的整体性能；应用研究进一步证明其诊断结果能为提升下游智能体成功率提供可操作的反馈。

Conclusion: TrajDebug 通过错误生命周期追踪有效解决了长轨迹中关键错误检测的两大核心挑战，其实验结果和应用价值表明该框架具有实用前景，代码和数据将开源以推动进一步研究。

Abstract: LLM-based agentic systems have shown remarkable capabilities in complex domains, while suffering from cascading errors and difficulty in debugging. Critical error detection aims to locate the earliest error step in a failed trajectory that is responsible for the final failure. However, progress faces two main challenges. First, long trajectories make it difficult to identify individual errors, since the evidence for judging a step may be scattered across distant instructions, observations, and prior context. Second, failed trajectories often contain multiple local errors with different downstream effects, only some of which remain responsible for the final failure. In this work, we propose TrajDebug, an error-lifecycle tracing framework that addresses long-trajectory error discovery with multi-granularity history compression and evidence-based error identification, and supports critical attribution by tracing each error's resolution status and terminal impact. We further construct TrajErrBench, a benchmark of 486 manually annotated failed trajectories from Tau2Bench and SWE-Bench Pro, covering realistic tool-use and coding scenarios. Experiments across diverse agent benchmarks show that TrajDebug achieves the best overall performance over existing baselines, and application studies further demonstrate that its diagnoses provide actionable feedback for improving downstream agent success. We will release the codes and data to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [354] [Challenges in Evaluating Explanation Methods for Static and Evolving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06351)
*Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对可解释人工智能（XAI）评估不足的问题，以DetoxAI图像识别系统为例，展示了偏差检测与概念遗忘，进而呈现了基于人类评估的图像分类解释方法，并探讨了在概念漂移数据流中自适应解释方法的挑战，特别是反事实解释的适应问题，最终关联到数据、模型与解释三者协同演化的追踪难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可解释人工智能（XAI）领域存在一个核心缺陷：评估（evaluation）不足。许多XAI方法缺乏充分的、尤其是基于人类判断的实证评估，导致其实际有效性和可信度存疑。本文旨在揭示这一问题，并探索面向动态数据流的解释方法演进。

Method: 本文采用多阶段的论证与实验方法：(1) 以DetoxAI图像识别系统为案例，展示XAI在偏差检测（bias detection）和概念遗忘（concept unlearning）中的评估局限；(2) 引入一项面向人类的评估（human-grounded evaluation）实例，用于评价图像分类解释方法；(3) 探索在概念漂移（concept drift）的数据流场景下，如何自适应地调整解释，重点讨论了反事实解释（counterfactuals）的适应经验；(4) 最终将上述问题纳入数据-模型-解释三者协同演化（co-evolution）的追踪框架中加以关联。

Result: 分析表明，DetoxAI案例清晰暴露了XAI评估的不充分性；人类参与的评估范式为解释方法提供了更可靠的验证路径；在概念漂移条件下，反事实解释需要随数据分布变化而动态调整，这带来了显著的工程与理论挑战；数据、模型与解释的协同演化追踪被识别为XAI领域的关键前瞻性问题。

Conclusion: XAI研究必须将严格的、以人为中心的评估作为核心环节。面向动态环境（如概念漂移），解释方法需要具备自适应能力，尤其是反事实解释需随数据演化而更新。未来XAI系统应建立数据、模型与解释三位一体的协同演化追踪机制，以确保解释在非平稳环境中的持续有效性与可信赖性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the limitations of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) with respect to insufficient evaluation. They are illustrated through the DetoxAI image recognition system for bias detection and concept unlearning. Then, an example of a human-grounded evaluation of methods for explaining image classification is presented. The paper further explores methods for adapting explanations to evolving data streams with concept drift. Experiences with adapting counterfactuals for this problem are discussed. Finally it is related to the challenges of tracking the co-evolution of data, models, and explanations.\footnote{This paper has been accepted for a publication in J.Nalepa (ed) Explainable AI in Space. Proceedings of EASi 2026 Workshop at IJCAI-ECAI 2026 Bremen, Springer CCIS vol 3107 (2016).}

</details>


### [355] [The Low Frequency Trap: Video Language Models Fail at Simple Event Bookkeeping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06361)
*Sarvesh Baskar,Zikui Cai,Shayan Shabihi,Anirudh Satheesh,Muhammad R. Islam,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于轨迹的参数量化评测方法，用于诊断视频模型在事件计数任务中的时序推理失败模式。通过在弹跳球碰撞、视觉闪烁和类别状态转换三个受控任务上对2190段视频进行系统测试，发现模型存在阶段性时序失败：事件表征类型决定模型能否初步获取视觉证据，且该缺陷随事件数量和频率增加而加剧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实世界视频基准覆盖面广，但固定片段将事件数量、速率、持续时间和视觉复杂度纠缠在一起，难以隔离失败模式。现有程序化基准虽提供更好控制，但仅评分最终答案，未能对照可执行的真实事件轨迹进行逐事件审计。因此需要一种能够精细诊断时序推理失败的方法。

Method: 提出"轨迹锚定参数量化评测"（trace-grounded parametric profiling）方法：设计弹跳球墙壁接触、视觉闪烁和类别状态转换三个受控视频任务，共生成2190段视频。系统性地改变事件数量N和频率F，同时保持渲染固定。每段视频配有一个可执行的事件轨迹，用于能力曲面估计和时间戳级别的逐事件评估。

Result: 在80%可靠性阈值下，Gemini 3.6 Flash对持续性状态转换在0.5和1.0 Hz下可可靠计数至12个事件，但对瞬时闪烁事件未显示出任何可靠的正确计数区域。在高计数高频率区间，仅0.2%的最终计数完全正确，模型仅能恢复18.1%的真实事件。提高采样率可将弹跳球任务准确率从19.6%提升至29.3%，但报告序列与真实轨迹一致的比例仅3.7%，额外帧可能虚增最终分数而未产生忠实的事件恢复。不同提示策略收益同样有限。

Conclusion: 轨迹锚定评测将视频评估从聚合准确率指标转变为对时序推理失败位置的详细诊断。事件表征决定了模型能否初步访问证据，这一局限随计数和频率增加而加剧。提高采样率虽可提升表面分数，但无法解决事件恢复的忠实性问题。

Abstract: Real-world video benchmarks provide broad coverage, but their fixed clips entangle event count, rate, duration, and visual complexity, making failure modes hard to isolate. While existing programmatic benchmarks offer better control, they score only the final answer rather than auditing reported events against executable ground truth. To bridge this gap, we introduce trace-grounded parametric profiling for event counting in three controlled video tasks: bouncing-ball wall contacts, visual blinks, and categorical state transitions. Across 2,190 videos, we vary event count N and frequency F while holding rendering fixed. Each video includes an executable event trace for capability-surface estimation and timestamp-level evaluation. Our results reveal a staged temporal failure. At an 80% reliability threshold, Gemini 3.6 Flash reliably counts persistent state transitions up to 12 events at 0.5 and 1.0 Hz, yet demonstrates no reliable positive-count region for transient blinking events. Thus, event representation dictates whether a model initially accesses evidence -- a limitation that compounds as count and frequency increase. In the high-count, high-frequency regime, only 0.2% of final counts are correct and the model recovers just 18.1% of true events. To test if visual access is the primary bottleneck, we increase sampling rate. Although this boosts Bounce Ball accuracy from 19.6% to 29.3%, the reported sequence agrees with ground truth only 3.7% of the time. Extra frames can therefore inflate final scores without producing faithful event recovery. Different prompting strategies yield similarly limited gains, and real-world video evaluations show the same concentration of success at low event counts. Ultimately, trace-grounded profiling shifts video evaluation from aggregate accuracy metrics to a detailed diagnostic of where temporal reasoning fails.

</details>


### [356] [Tracing the Heart: An Evidence-Linked Pipeline for Heart-Failure Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06366)
*Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Michelle Hu,Dorisa Shehi,Daniel Kang,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Koustuv Saha,Christian Poellabauer,Christopher Lee,Sajeev Singh,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar,Zeeshan Ahmed,Priyadarshini Kachroo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 Nimblemind 多智能体系统（nMAS），一个用于心力衰竭 EHR 特征工程的自动化流水线，结合证据链接与评分量表，在 500 份虚拟病历上生成 132 个结构化特征和 70 个聚合特征，显著提升了 HFrEF 和 HFpEF 表型分类的 AUROC，并通过 LLM 审计验证了特征的方法学质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR 特征工程占数据科学家 39-45% 的工作量，是临床研究和 AI 的主要瓶颈。心力衰竭影响约 670 万美国成年人，需要整合碎片化的 EHR 数据并进行基于指南的临床推理。现有基于规则和 LLM 的方法仅提供部分自动化，且可维护性和证据可追溯性不足。

Method: 开发 nMAS（Nimblemind Multi-Agent System），一个证据链接、量表驱动的自动化流水线。在 9 张 EHR 源表的 500 份虚拟患者记录上进行评估：生成 132 个结构化特征和 70 个经量表评分的聚合特征，验证结构完整性、量表合规性和数据溯源，并由受限 LLM 进行审计。

Result: 加入聚合特征后，HFrEF 表型的留出集 AUROC 从 0.895 提升至 0.963，HFpEF 从 0.870 提升至 0.910。独立 LLM 对证据支持和方法学严谨性的量表评估得分为满分的 81.5%。

Conclusion: 结果证明了复杂心血管 EHR 数据自动化、可审计特征工程的可行性，但评估仅限于单机构队列，需要外部验证。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) feature engineering is a major bottleneck in clinical research and AI, accounting for 39-45% of data scientists' workload. This is especially pronounced in heart failure, which affects an estimated 6.7 million U.S. adults and requires integrating fragmented EHR data with disease-specific, guideline-based clinical reasoning. Existing rule-based and large language model (LLM)-based approaches offer only partial automation with limited maintainability and evidence traceability. We developed the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), an evidence-linked, rubric-grounded pipeline for automated heart-failure feature engineering, and evaluated it on 500 dummy patient records from nine EHR source tables. nMAS generated 132 structured and 70 rubric-scored aggregated features, verified for structural integrity, rubric compliance, and provenance, and audited by a restricted LLM. Adding the aggregated features improved held-out AUROC from 0.895 to 0.963 for HFrEF and 0.870 to 0.910 for HFpEF phenotyping, and an independent LLM-based rubric assessment of evidence support and methodological soundness scored the features at 81.5% of maximum points. These results demonstrate the feasibility of automated, auditable feature engineering for complex cardiovascular EHR data, though evaluation was limited to a single-institution cohort and external validation is needed.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [357] [Improving the efficiency of infectious disease prevention trials using negative control outcome event times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05261)
*Ethan Ashby,Zhewei Zhang,Tanya P. Garcia,Ting Ye,Holly Janes,Bo Zhang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出在传染病预防随机试验中，利用阴性对照结局（NCO）事件时间进行协变量调整，以提高治疗效果估计的精度。由于病原体暴露是关键的预后因素但基线难以测量，传统协变量调整效果有限。作者推导了双删失情形下的有效影响函数，构建了交叉拟合一步估计量，并通过数值实验和HIV-1预防试验（HVTN 704/HPTN 085）验证了方法的有效性——调整细菌性性传播感染时间使预防效力估计的方差降低了约27%，远优于基线协变量调整的约2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在疫苗或被动抗体等传染病预防随机试验中，个体对病原体的暴露程度是主要的预后因素，但在基线时几乎无法测量。因此，常规的基线协变量调整带来的精度增益十分有限。需要一种新的调整策略，利用试验期间可观测、且与暴露机制共享但不受干预因果影响的变量来提升估计效率。

Method: 作者提出调整阴性对照结局（NCO）事件时间——该结局不受干预因果影响，但与主要结局共享暴露机制。首先形式化了NCO事件时间调整有效的假设条件，并处理NCO右删失带来的复杂性。推导了主要结局与NCO事件时间均右删失时，治疗组别特异性生存函数的有效影响函数（EIF），并基于此构建了交叉拟合的一步估计量（cross-fitted one-step estimator），该估计量具有多重稳健性且当冗余参数估计准确时达到渐近有效。

Result: 数值实验表明：当NCO事件时间不具信息性时，所提估计量与基准方法表现相当；当NCO事件时间对主要结局预后性更强时，精度显著提升。在HVTN 704/HPTN 085（VRC01广谱中和抗体预防HIV-1的随机双盲试验）的实际应用中，调整细菌性性传播感染（HIV-1的阴性对照结局）时间后，预防效力估计的方差降低约27%，而传统基线协变量调整仅降低约2.5%。

Conclusion: NCO事件时间调整是一种在传染病预防试验中显著提升估计效率的有效策略。通过利用与主要结局共享暴露机制但不受干预影响的阴性对照结局，可以捕捉基线协变量无法反映的暴露异质性，从而大幅降低治疗效应估计的方差。该方法具有多重稳健性和渐近有效性，在HIV预防试验中展现出远超传统方法的实际价值。

Abstract: Baseline covariate adjustment can enhance the efficiency of randomized trials by improving precision of treatment effect estimates. However, the precision gain depends on how strongly the baseline covariates are prognostic for the primary outcome. In randomized trials of infectious disease prevention interventions (e.g., vaccines or passively administered antibodies), an individual's exposure to the pathogen is a leading prognostic factor but is rarely measurable at baseline. Hence, conventional covariate adjustment offers limited precision gain in prevention trials. We propose adjusting for a negative control outcome (NCO) event time, which is causally unaffected by the intervention but shares overlapping exposure mechanisms with the primary outcome. We formalize assumptions under which adjustment for the NCO event time is valid, and show that right-censoring of the NCO event time further complicates adjustment. We derive the efficient influence function for the treatment-arm-specific survivor function of the primary outcome when both the primary outcome and the NCO event time are right-censored, and use it to construct a cross-fitted, one-step estimator that is multiply robust to nuisance misspecification and asymptotically efficient when the nuisances are estimated accurately. In numerical experiments, our estimator compares comparably to benchmarks when the NCO event time is uninformative, and gains precision as the NCO event time is more prognostic for the primary outcome. We apply our method to HVTN 704/HPTN 085, a randomized, double-blinded trial of VRC01, a broadly neutralizing antibody against HIV-1. Adjusting for the time to a bacterial sexually transmitted infection --- a negative control outcome for HIV-1 acquisition --- reduced the estimated variance of the prevention efficacy estimate by approximately 27%, compared to roughly 2.5% for baseline covariate adjustment.

</details>


### [358] [Precision and Decisiveness as Goals: Reliable Sequential Hypothesis Testing with a Dual Stopping Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05301)
*Eyal A. Kazin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: DPitG 在 PitG 的精度停时规则基础上增加了必须同时满足判决条件的要求，大幅降低了无结论率（从62%降至2%），且保持零假阳性，仅以中位5%的额外样本量为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序贯检验方法存在两难：HDI+ROPE 因停时规则与决策准则耦合导致早期偷看产生确认偏误和假阳性；PitG 虽通过完全解耦消除了该偏误，但常因仅停于精度达标而无法得出明确结论，尤其在零假设为真时无结论率极高。因此需要一种既能保证判决可靠性又能维持高结论率的方法。

Method: 提出 DPitG（Decisive Precision is the Goal），要求同时满足两个条件才停止：① HDI 宽度达到预设精度目标 ω；② HDI 完全落在 ROPE 之外（拒绝零假设）或完全落在 ROPE 之内（接受零假设）。给出了闭式规划公式 N ∝ V/ω²，提供了在线交互式计算器和开源代码。以公平硬币模拟（ω=0.08, ROPE=0.5±0.05）和不同 ω 范围进行验证，并与 HDI+ROPE 和 PitG 进行对比。

Result: 在公平硬币模拟中，DPitG 将 PitG 的无结论率从 62% 降至 2%，中位样本量仅增加 5%，且假阳性为零；HDI+ROPE 要达到类似结论率需付出假阳性代价。在测试的 ω 范围内，DPitG 结论率保持在 97% 以上，而 PitG 波动很大。DPitG 的停时规则完全可预注册，适用于单组二值数据，并可扩展至连续结局和两组比较。

Conclusion: DPitG 在保持零假阳性的前提下，以极小的额外样本成本将 PitG 的无结论率降低至可忽略水平，兼具可靠判决与高结论率。其完全预指定的停时规则符合预注册标准，是在需要可靠判决场景下的方法首选。

Abstract: Sequential hypothesis testing offers flexibility over fixed-sample designs, but stopping rules coupled to decision criteria risk confirmation bias through early peeking. The HDI+ROPE algorithm exemplifies this: it stops as soon as the posterior Highest Density Interval (HDI) falls entirely outside a Region of Practical Equivalence (ROPE) around the null (rejecting it) or entirely inside, which enables positive acceptance of the null, something Null Hypothesis Significance Testing cannot do. However, this coupling means a wide HDI can satisfy the criterion on early, unrepresentative samples, incurring a systematic false-positive cost. Fully decoupled methods, such as ``Precision is the Goal'' (PitG), eliminate this bias by halting only once a target HDI width $ω$ is reached; yet, by divorcing the stopping rule from the decision criterion, PitG frequently yields inconclusive outcomes, particularly when the null hypothesis is true. We propose ``Decisive Precision is the Goal'' (DPitG), which requires both the precision target and a conclusive verdict to be satisfied simultaneously. In fair coin simulations ($ω=0.08$, ROPE$=0.5\pm0.05$), DPitG reduces the PitG inconclusive rate from 62% to 2% at a median cost of only 5% more samples, with zero false positives; HDI+ROPE achieves comparable conclusiveness only at the cost of false positives. Across the $ω$ range tested, DPitG conclusiveness remains above 97% while PitG's varies widely. We provide a closed-form planning formula $N\propto V/ω^2$ (where $V$ is the observation variance), an online interactive calculator, and open-source code. Demonstrated on single-group binary data, the framework extends readily to continuous outcomes and two-group comparisons. DPitG's fully pre-specified stopping rule is compatible with pre-registration standards and is the method of choice whenever a reliable verdict is required.

</details>


### [359] [Joint Spatial and Temporal Generalized Dissimilarity Mixed Modeling (stGDMM) for Beta Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05352)
*Philip A. White,Henry A. Frye,Jasper A. Slingsby,John A. Silander,,Hana Petersen,Alan E. Gelfand*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文在先前空间广义相异混合模型（spGDMM）的基础上，将其扩展至时空动态建模框架，利用Bray-Curtis相异性度量对beta多样性进行时空联合分析，并以南非开普植物区（CFR）数据集进行实证说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础GDM存在随机性问题，且先前的spGDMM仅考虑空间维度，未能捕捉beta多样性在时间维度上的动态变化。因此需要将模型扩展至时空动态框架，以揭示更丰富的生态格局。

Method: 在spGDMM的基础上引入时间动态成分，构建时空广义相异混合模型。该模型在空间-时间对的产品空间上运作，使用Bray-Curtis相异性指数作为beta多样性的度量，同时对空间和时间维度上的物种组成差异进行统计建模。

Result: 时空扩展后的模型相比纯空间模型提供了更丰富的建模可能性，能够有效捕捉beta多样性在时间和空间上的联合变化模式。应用南非开普植物区数据验证了该方法的实用性和解释力。

Conclusion: 将spGDMM扩展至时空动态框架显著增强了广义相异模型的分析能力，为理解beta多样性的时空演变规律提供了更全面的统计工具。

Abstract: Generalized dissimilarity models (GDMs) have emerged as a valuable tool for formal statistical analysis of biodiversity. In particular, beta diversity, measured using dissimilarity measures, e.g., the Bray-Curtis dissimilarity in our case, provides a statistical summary of the difference in species composition between sites. It also provides novel data for spatial and spatio-temporal modeling as it resides over the product space of one space-time pair and a second space-time pair. In earlier work we developed the spatial generalized dissimilarity mixed model (spGDMM) to remedy some of the stochastic issues concerned with the foundational GDM in the literature. Here, we extend that work to include dynamics. We find much richer modeling opportunities as we consider beta diversity with regard to change in time as well as space. We illustrate with a dataset from the Cape Floristic Region (CFR) in South Africa.

</details>


### [360] [Sparse Principal Component Analysis via Wavelets for Distributed Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05386)
*Giovanni Barbosa Herrero,Rodney Vasconcelos Fonseca,Aluísio Pinheiro*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将Johnstone & Lu (2009)的小波稀疏化技术与Fan et al. (2019)的分布式PCA框架相结合的方法，在联邦学习场景下实现高维主成分分析，在降低通信成本的同时，当维度超过一定阈值时估计误差优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习场景中数据分布在多台机器上且无法直接汇总，需进行分布式统计推断。Johnstone & Lu (2009)指出PCA在高维状态下统计不一致，并利用小波稀疏化恢复一致性；Fan et al. (2019)虽实现了分布式下的特征空间估计，但未处理高维情形。本文旨在填补这一空白：在高维分布式场景下实现稀疏化PCA，在保证估计质量的同时降低通信成本。

Method: 将Johnstone & Lu (2009)中基于小波的稀疏化变量选择策略嵌入Fan et al. (2019)的分布式PCA框架中。具体而言，利用小波变换对高维数据进行稀疏表示，仅传输稀疏化后的小波系数进行跨机器通信与聚合，从而在分布式高维场景下一致地估计汇聚数据的特征空间。

Result: 在维度 d ∈ [52, 5000] 的模拟实验中，当维度超过明确阈值后（λ=25 时 d ≥ 152，λ=50 时 d ≥ 252），所提方法的估计误差低于Fan et al. (2019)；同时，在整个研究维度范围内，该方法系统性地传输了更少的系数。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功将小波稀疏化引入分布式PCA，在高维场景下以更低的通信成本实现了优于Fan et al. (2019)的特征空间估计精度，验证了稀疏化策略在联邦高维数据分析中的有效性。

Abstract: The large volume of data and concerns about data privacy have motivated the development of techniques for distributed data, a problem also known as federated learning. In this scenario, sub-samples of the data are divided across different machines, and statistics must be computed over that data without direct access to the full sample. Johnstone & Lu (2009, JASA) show that principal component analysis (PCA) is statistically inconsistent in the high-dimensional regime, and propose a way to recover consistency through wavelet-based sparsification and variable selection. Fan et al. (2019, AoS) show a way to perform this same estimation -- specifically, to estimate the eigenspace that would be obtained if all the data were pooled together, even though it remains effectively distributed -- without addressing the high-dimensional regime. This work incorporates the wavelet-based sparsification of Johnstone & Lu (2009) into the distributed PCA framework of Fan et al. (2019), aiming to reduce communication cost without compromising the quality of the eigenspace estimation. Simulations across $d \in [52, 5000]$ show that the proposed method overtakes Fan et al. (2019) in estimation error beyond a clear dimensional threshold ($d \geq 152$ for $λ=25$, $d \geq 252$ for $λ=50$), while transmitting systematically fewer coefficients throughout the entire range studied. This study was financed by the Sao Paulo Research Foundation (FAPESP), Brazil. Process Number #2023/02538-0 and Number #2025/21250-2.

</details>


### [361] [Inference for subgraph densities in noisy dynamic networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05407)
*Peter W. MacDonald,Eric D. Kolaczyk*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对动态网络序列的子图密度统计推断方法，能够显式校正边观测误差，并在网络规模增长时具备良好的理论性质。通过引入随机演化的隐马尔可夫网络模型，该方法解决了非相同网络复制品的鲁棒性和多快照高效聚合两大问题，并扩展到多时间点的联合推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chang et al. (2022) 指出了两个有待进一步研究的关键方向：(1) 对非相同网络复制品的鲁棒性；(2) 多个网络快照的高效聚合。动态网络序列中，网络复制品常随时间被观测，现有方法难以同时应对观测误差与动态演化的双重挑战。

Method: 作者构建了一个随机演化的隐马尔可夫网络模型（stochastically evolving hidden Markov network model），在此基础上开发子图密度估计与推断方法，显式校正网络边的观测误差，并将方法扩展至多时间点下的子图密度联合推断，以便进行动态网络快照的正式统计比较。

Result: 所提方法在网络规模增长时具备良好的理论性质，能够有效应对非相同网络复制品场景，并实现对多个网络快照的高效聚合，显著拓展了含噪网络分析的数据适用范围。

Conclusion: 该研究通过隐马尔可夫框架统一解决了动态网络中子图密度估计的观测误差校正、非相同复制品鲁棒性及多快照聚合问题，为动态网络序列的统计推断提供了新的方法论工具。

Abstract: In this work we develop statistical methodology to estimate and perform inference on subgraph densities using time-indexed, or dynamic network sequences. These estimates explicitly adjust for observation errors for the network edges, and have good theoretical properties as the size of the network grows. By specifying a stochastically evolving hidden Markov network model, we address two important directions for further investigation identified by Chang et al. (2022): robustness to non-identical network replicates, and efficient aggregation of multiple available network snapshots. These new methods vastly expand the analysis of noisy networks to new data settings, as network replicates are commonly observed dynamically. The methodology is also extended to consider joint inference for subgraph densities at multiple time points, to facilitate formal statistical comparison of dynamic network snapshots.

</details>


### [362] [Risk-Aware Quantile Learning for Personalized Dynamic Treatment Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05434)
*Chunyin Lei,Annie Qu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出风险感知分位数动态治疗方案（RQDTR），在传统分位数DTR基础上，同时纳入治疗相关风险控制与多类别治疗决策，通过角度基公式统一学习多治疗规则，并在理论上建立了一致性与误差界，在抑郁和脓毒症数据中展现出更优的尾部疗效与获益-风险权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序贯临床决策往往需要在优化疗效的同时控制治疗相关风险，并处理多类别治疗选择。现有分位数动态治疗方案（DTR）方法仅关注疗效优化且局限于二值治疗，未能同时兼顾分布尾部特征与风险控制。

Method: 提出RQDTR统一框架：(1) 优化累积潜在结果的预设分位数，同时显式纳入治疗相关风险；(2) 开发角度基公式（angle-based formulation）实现多类别治疗规则的联合学习；(3) 包含三个可解释子类：纯疗效分位数学习、群体水平风险控制的约束学习、以及基于复合获益-风险效用的效用学习。理论上建立了识别与神谕等价性、平滑代理的Fisher一致性、估计方案的一致性及有限样本误差率。

Result: 在All of Us重度抑郁障碍数据和MIMIC-III脓毒症数据上的广泛模拟与应用表明，RQDTR相比现有分位数DTR方法，显著改善了尾部导向的疗效，并实现了更优的获益-风险权衡。

Conclusion: RQDTR成功将风险感知机制与多类别决策统一到分位数DTR框架中，为临床序贯决策提供了更全面的优化工具，在理论和实证层面均验证了其有效性与优越性。

Abstract: Sequential clinical decision-making often involves more than maximizing average efficacy. Clinicians may need to simultaneously optimize clinically relevant tails of the outcome distribution, control treatment-related risk, and choose among multiple treatment options. Existing quantile dynamic treatment regime (DTR) methods capture distributional features of treatment outcomes but remain largely restricted to efficacy-only objectives and binary treatments. To address these limitations, we propose Risk-Aware Quantile Dynamic Treatment Regimes (RQDTR), a unified framework that optimizes a prespecified quantile of the cumulative potential outcome while explicitly incorporating treatment-related risk. We also develop an angle-based formulation for jointly learning decision rules across multiple treatment categories. Our framework includes three interpretable subclasses: efficacy-only quantile learning, constraint-based learning with population-level risk control, and utility-based learning through a composite benefit-risk utility. Theoretically, we establish identification and oracle equivalence, Fisher consistency of the smoothed surrogate, consistency of the estimated regime, and finite-sample performance error rates. Extensive simulation studies and applications to All of Us major depressive disorder and MIMIC-III sepsis data demonstrate that RQDTR improves tail-oriented efficacy and achieves more favorable benefit-risk trade-offs than existing quantile DTR methods.

</details>


### [363] [Fast Power Evaluation under Biased-Coin Minimization: Sampling and Randomization Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05527)
*Masahiro Kojima*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出可复用的分层不平衡高斯近似（SIGA）框架，将固定得分统计量精确分解为跨层不平衡分量和层内正交分量，并在分配-复制限制条件下开发了SIGA-S（重复抽样校准）和SIGA-R（随机化校准）两种程序，大幅降低了有偏硬币最小化下设计阶段功效与样本量评估的计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在设计阶段，基于有偏硬币最小化的功效和样本量评估需要在每次模拟试验中重复执行预设的随机化检验，计算成本极高。现有方法缺乏一种既能保持统计精度又能显著降低计算负担的可复用框架。

Method: 作者提出SIGA框架，核心是将固定得分统计量精确分解为跨层不平衡分量和层内正交分量。在此基础上，开发两种校准程序：(1) SIGA-S：在相同绝对不平衡规则的显式分配-复制限制条件下，于边际均值或风险差边界处一致估计重复抽样方差；(2) SIGA-R：引入一阶方差间隙（以涉及配对路径协方差的二次型表示），通过可复用的配对分配校准来近似条件参考分布，刻画非尖锐边界下条件方差的差异。

Result: 全面的基准测试表明，SIGA-S和SIGA-R分别与对应的参考随机化检验高度吻合。基于已发表汇总规划特征的试验模拟同样显示，SIGA-S、SIGA-R与参考随机化检验的功效相近，但两种可复用校准程序相较嵌套重随机化大幅减少了计算量。

Conclusion: SIGA框架通过精确分解和可复用校准，在保持统计推断精度的前提下，有效解决了有偏硬币最小化设计阶段功效评估的计算瓶颈，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Design-stage power and sample-size evaluation under biased-coin minimization can be computationally intensive when a prespecified randomization test is reproduced within every simulated trial. We develop a reusable stratum-imbalance Gaussian approximation (SIGA) framework by exactly decomposing a fixed-score statistic into joint-stratum imbalance and orthogonal within-stratum components. Under explicit allocation-copy limit conditions for the same absolute-imbalance rule, the sampling-calibrated procedure, SIGA-S, consistently estimates the repeated-sampling variance at a marginal mean- or risk-difference boundary. At a nonsharp boundary, the conditional variance of a fixed-score randomization test can differ because the score contains the observed allocation path. To characterize this distinction, we express the first-order variance gap as a quadratic form involving pair-path covariance and introduce the randomization-calibrated procedure, SIGA-R, based on a reusable paired allocation-only calibration to approximate the conditional reference distribution. Separate comprehensive benchmarks showed close agreement between each SIGA procedure and the corresponding reference randomization test. A trial-inspired simulation based on published aggregate planning characteristics likewise produced similar power for SIGA-S, SIGA-R and the reference randomization test, while both reusable calibration procedures substantially reduced computation relative to nested rerandomization.

</details>


### [364] [Two base rates, two weights: base-rate neglect has a second axis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05658)
*Adam Y. Shavit*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出基率忽视并非单一错误，而是包含两种独立的校正：对先验概率（结果基率）的低估和对线索本身频率（线索基率）的低估。前者是经典基率忽视，后者是偶然性学习中的线索密度效应。作者将两种校正统一纳入一个贝叶斯方程，分别对应两个权重，并论证两者可被独立操纵，构成双重分离。该框架的核心预测尚待实验检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将基率忽视视为单一错误——对先验概率给予过低权重。但作者指出，从共现中形成判断需要同时校正两个基率：结果的基率（先验）和线索本身的基率。后者在偶然性学习中被称为"线索密度效应"，但从未被认定为一种基率忽视。作者旨在统一这两种忽视，揭示现有学习与记忆模型中被掩盖的分歧，并提出可实验检验的双重分离预测。

Method: 作者将两种基率校正纳入一个统一的贝叶斯方程，分别用两个权重参数表示：一个权重对应先验（结果基率）的校正程度，另一个权重对应线索频率的校正程度。通过分析，作者展示了这两个权重在极端取值下可以还原出信号检测论标准、效度/敏感性/邻近性三联体、因果强度的"lift"测度等已知量。作者还指出，线索频率权重被嵌入六种标准学习与记忆模型中，但常规实验因采用二择一测验而掩盖了该权重的效应。核心方法论贡献是提出通过分级评分任务（而非二择一任务）来揭示线索频率权重，并设计可独立操纵两种基率的实验实现双重分离。

Result: 分析表明：（1）基率忽视应被视为两个独立的过程——低估先验（经典基率忽视）和低估线索频率（线索密度效应）；（2）线索频率权重仅在分级评分任务中可被测量，二择一测验会将其抵消；（3）该框架在极端条件下可还原出多个已知的心理量；（4）六种看似一致的学习记忆模型，实际上因常规实验范式压缩数据形式而掩盖了分歧；（5）两种基率忽视应可被独立操纵，构成双重分离，这是该框架的核心预测，且尚未被检验。

Conclusion: 基率忽视应被重新概念化为两种独立错误的组合：低估结果先验和低估线索频率。该双权重框架统一了多个研究传统中的已知量，揭示了现有模型间被掩盖的分歧，并给出了可检验的双重分离预测。本文阐述了理论框架和关键的评分实验设计，配套论文则将双权重模型拟合至已有的颜色-味道数据集。

Abstract: Base-rate neglect is usually treated as one mistake: giving the prior too little weight. Turning the co-occurrences you see into a useful judgment, though, means correcting for two base rates, not one. The first is the familiar prior, how common the outcome is. The second is how common the cue itself is. Those are two separate mistakes, and a learner can make either one alone. Under-correcting the prior is classical base-rate neglect; under-correcting the cue is the cue-density effect of contingency learning, long studied but not previously recognised as a kind of base-rate neglect. We write both corrections as two weights in one Bayesian equation. The task decides which weight it can measure: the cue-frequency weight appears only in graded ratings, because a two-choice test cancels it. At their extremes the two weights recover familiar quantities: base-rate neglect, the signal-detection criterion, the contiguity/sensitivity/validity triple, and the "lift" measure of causal strength. The same cue-frequency weight also sits inside six standard learning-and-memory models; they seem to agree, but only because the usual experiments squeeze the data into a form where they cannot disagree. Above all, the two neglects should be separately manipulable: an experimenter can move one without moving the other. That is a double dissociation, and a one-parameter account cannot produce it. That prediction is the framework's centre, and it has not yet been tested. This paper lays out the framework and the rating experiment that would settle it; a companion paper fits the two weights to an existing colour-flavour dataset.

</details>


### [365] [Multiparametric MRI Radiomics and Machine Learning Framework for Predicting Treatment Response in Glioblastoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05733)
*Suchibrata Patra*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于DCE-MRI体素级简约药代动力学模型的影像组学方法，结合MGMT状态，用于区分GBM放化疗后的真性进展(TP)与假性进展(PsP)。Random Forest分类器联合简约DCE-MRI影像组学与MGMT状态达到AUC 0.89、敏感性0.93、特异性0.76，显著优于传统T1增强影像(0.72)和单一扩展Tofts模型(0.68)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GBM患者在放化疗后，真性进展(TP)与假性进展(PsP)在常规增强MRI上表现几乎一致，但两者临床管理策略截然不同，现有常规影像手段无法可靠区分，亟需一种无创、可精准鉴别的诊断方法。

Method: 纳入82例IDH野生型GBM患者(53例TP，29例PsP)，均在放化疗后6个月内出现新增强病灶。在体素级别对DCE-MRI时间-浓度曲线拟合五种候选药代动力学模型，通过AIC最小化选择最优模型，生成简约的Ktrans、Ve、Vp、taui参数图。分割后提取1073个影像组学特征，经Mann-Whitney U检验筛选和Elastic Net降维，使用五种分类器在四种特征配置下训练。最优方案为Random Forest结合简约DCE-MRI影像组学与MGMT状态。

Result: Random Forest联合简约DCE-MRI影像组学与MGMT状态：AUC 0.89、敏感性0.93、特异性0.76、F1 0.90。无MGMT特征时为0.84；T1增强基线仅为0.72；单一扩展Tofts模型为0.68。Ktrans图的形状和纹理特征以及肿瘤体积是最强预测因子，MGMT提供较小但独立的贡献。

Conclusion: 体素级自适应模型选择优于单一固定药代动力学模型，联合MGMT状态可进一步提升无创鉴别TP与PsP的性能，为GBM个体化治疗决策提供影像生物标志物支撑。

Abstract: Distinguishing True Progression (TP) from Pseudo-Progression (PsP) after chemoradiotherapy remains a major diagnostic challenge in GBM, as both entities present near-identical appearances on conventional contrast-enhanced post-treatment MRI. This distinction carries substantial clinical weight, since TP and PsP demand divergent management yet cannot be reliably separated on routine imaging alone. We investigated whether radiomic features derived from a parsimonious, voxel-wise pharmacokinetic model of dynamic contrast-enhanced (DCE) MRI, combined with MGMT status, could discriminate between the two. The cohort comprised 82 adults with IDH-wildtype GBM who developed a new contrast-enhancing lesion within six months of chemoradiotherapy; classification (53 TP, 29 PsP) was established by histopathology where available (n=52) and modified RANO criteria otherwise (n=30). At every voxel, contrast-concentration time courses were fitted to five candidate pharmacokinetic models, and the best fit was retained by AIC minimisation, yielding parsimonious Ktrans, Ve, Vp, and taui maps adapting to local heterogeneity rather than a single fixed model across the tumour. Following segmentation, 1,073 radiomic descriptors were extracted and reduced via Mann-Whitney U filtering and Elastic Net, then used to train five classifiers across four feature configurations. A Random Forest classifier combining parsimonious DCE-MRI radiomics with MGMT status achieved the best discrimination (mean AUC 0.89, sensitivity 0.93, specificity 0.76, F1 0.90), outperforming features without MGMT (0.84), a T1-post-contrast baseline (0.72), and a single-model extended-Tofts analysis (0.68). Shape and textural descriptors of the Ktrans map, with tumour volume, were the strongest predictors, MGMT contributing a smaller, independent effect. Allowing the model to vary voxel-wise improves non-invasive discrimination.

</details>


### [366] [Fuzzy network jump models for soft dynamic clustering of graph-structured data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05786)
*Federico P. Cortese*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种模糊网络跳跃模型，用于对加权图节点上时变观测数据进行聚类，通过空间-时间正则化促进相邻节点和连续时间点之间平滑的软聚类分配，并采用高效交替优化进行估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要对索引于加权图节点的时变观测数据进行聚类，现有方法在同时处理空间依赖（图结构）和时间平滑性方面存在不足，需一种能灵活结合图表示并进行时空正则化的模糊聚类框架。

Method: 提出模糊网络跳跃模型（fuzzy network jump model），在图结构上引入空间正则化和时间正则化，促进软聚类分配在连接节点和相邻时间点间的平滑性；估计采用利用正则化项二次结构的交替优化方案。

Result: 模拟研究表明，该方法在不同空间依赖程度和聚类重叠水平下均能准确恢复真实隶属概率，且优于其他竞争聚类方法；在旧金山市交通网络数据应用中，识别出可解释的交通模式，并揭示了其在时间和相连路段上的演化。

Conclusion: 所提模糊网络跳跃模型能有效对图节点时变数据进行聚类，兼具良好的统计精度与可解释性，在模拟和真实交通网络数据上均表现优越。

Abstract: We introduce a fuzzy network jump model for clustering time-varying observations indexed by the nodes of a weighted graph. The framework allows flexible graph representations with spatial and temporal regularization promoting smooth soft cluster assignments across connected nodes and consecutive time points. Estimation is performed through an efficient alternating optimization scheme that exploits the quadratic structure of the regularization terms. A simulation study covering different levels of spatial dependence and cluster overlap shows that the proposed method accurately recovers the true membership probabilities and outperforms competing clustering methods. An application to traffic-network data for the city of San Francisco identifies interpretable traffic regimes and reveals their evolution over time and across connected road segments.

</details>


### [367] [Joint Multiple Imputation of Node Attributes and Network Ties in R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05895)
*Robert W. Krause*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: netimpute是一个R包，通过链式方程联合填补社会网络中的缺失节点属性和缺失网络关系。它计算大量节点级结构/同质性度量，利用PCA降维，采用类MR-QAP的二元回归模型填补关系，并使用Gibbs抽样顺序更新每条缺失边，从而同时处理属性和网络结构的缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缺失数据处理方法存在割裂：标准多重填补软件（如mice）能处理节点属性缺失但忽略网络结构信息，而专门的网络填补方法则将关系填补和属性填补视为独立问题。需要一种统一框架，使属性填补能利用网络结构信息，关系填补能利用属性同质性信息，实现二者协同填补。

Method: netimpute包包含四个核心组件：(a) 计算跨网络的节点级结构度量和属性同质性度量；(b) 通过PCA降维并保留特定假设相关的原始度量；(c) 提供类MR-QAP的二元回归模型用于网络关系建模；(d) 通过netmice()实现链式方程联合填补，在网络和属性之间循环迭代：用网络导出的预测变量填补属性，用属性导出（及其他网络导出）的预测变量填补关系。网络关系采用逐条Gibbs更新，每次重抽一条缺失边，条件依赖于所有已填补的边，并通过变化统计量实时更新内生统计量（互惠性、共享联系人）。包还支持结构零值、网络间逻辑约束、社会关系模型随机截距、含交互项的自定义填补模型，以及网络感知的按目标预测变量选择（netquickpred()）。

Result: 论文提供了可复现示例展示包的用法，涵盖二元和加权网络的多种结构度量计算，并演示了netmice()的联合填补流程。

Conclusion: netimpute填补了现有软件在联合处理网络数据中节点属性缺失和关系缺失方面的空白，通过将网络结构信息融入属性填补、将属性同质性信息融入关系填补的循环机制，为网络数据的多重填补提供了统一、灵活且统计上更为合理的解决方案。

Abstract: Missing data in social network studies routinely affects both node-level attributes and the network ties themselves. Standard multiple imputation software such as mice (van Buuren and Groothuis-Oudshoorn, 2011) handles the former well but has no notion of network structure, while dedicated network-imputation procedures typically treat tie imputation and attribute imputation as separate problems. This paper introduces netimpute, an R package that (a) computes a broad battery of node-level structural and attribute homophily measures across one or more networks, (b) can reduce these to a manageable set of principal component predictors while optionally preserving specific raw measures that are themselves part of substantive hypotheses, (c) provides a dyadic (cell-level) regression for network ties in the spirit of MR-QAP (Krackhardt, 1988), and (d) combines both into netmice(), a chained-equations routine that jointly imputes missing node attributes and missing network ties by cycling between them, using network-derived predictors for attributes and attribute-derived (and other-network-derived) predictors for ties. Network ties are updated tie-wise by default: a sequential Gibbs step redraws each missing tie one at a time, conditional on all previously imputed ties, refreshing the endogenous statistics (reciprocity, shared contacts) after every single draw via change statistics. The package additionally supports structural zeros, logical constraints between networks, social-relations-model random intercepts in the tie model, custom imputation models with interactions among any internally created terms, and network-aware per-target predictor selection (netquickpred()). We describe the package's design choices, the measures it computes for binary and non-negative weighted networks, and illustrate its use with a reproducible example

</details>


### [368] [A Regression-Based Framework for the ACF, PACF, Durbin-Levinson Recursion, and One-Step-Ahead Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06334)
*Kellen Gong,Fang Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于回归的统一教学框架，将自相关函数、偏自相关函数、Durbin-Levinson递推及一步超前预测均重新表述为普通最小二乘回归的结果，从而弥合回归分析与时间序列课程之间的概念鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统教学中，ACF和PACF常被当作计算配方教授，与学生在回归课程中已建立的回归框架脱节，导致概念壁垒。作者旨在用学生熟悉的回归语言重新解释这些时间序列核心工具。

Method: 将ACF重新解释为平稳过程对其自身滞后项的一元线性回归系数；将PACF解释为逐步扩展的多元回归模型中最新滞后项的系数；将Durbin-Levinson递推视为一系列偏回归，每一步均可用OLS恒等式表达。通过MA(1)和AR(1)过程的工作示例加以说明。

Result: MA(1)和AR(1)示例展示：AR(1)的ACF呈指数衰减（拖尾）、PACF在滞后1处截尾；MA(1)的ACF在滞后1处截尾、PACF呈几何衰减（拖尾）——这些特征直接从回归视角自然得出。

Conclusion: 采用回归驱动的教学框架能显著降低学生学习时间序列分析的认知门槛，使ACF、PACF和Durbin-Levinson递推变得直观透明。作者提供了具体的课堂教学建议。

Abstract: The autocorrelation function (ACF) and partial autocorrelation function (PACF) are foundational tools for identifying autoregressive moving-average (ARMA) models, yet they are often introduced to students as computational recipes disconnected from the regression framework students already know. This note develops a unified, regression-based instructional framework for the ACF, the PACF, the Durbin-Levinson recursion, and one-step-ahead prediction in stationary time series. We show that the ACF is precisely the coefficient from a simple linear regression of a stationary process on one of its own lags, that the PACF is the coefficient on the newest lag in an expanding multiple regression model, and that the Durbin-Levinson recursion is nothing more than a sequence of partial regressions, each expressible using ordinary least-squares identities from a first course in regression. Worked examples for the MA(1) and AR(1) processes illustrate how the tailing-off and cutting-off behavior of the ACF and PACF emerge directly from this viewpoint. We argue that this framing lowers the conceptual barrier between regression courses and time series courses and provide classroom-oriented suggestions for instruction.

</details>
